   oṭuṅkum - (oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy) oṭuṅki (āṉmāvai) iṉpuṟuttum,
   karuttiṉai aṟivittu - (karuttai aṭiyēṉ) aṟiya aṟivittu (eṉṟavāṟu).
   nilam mutal nātam īṟāṉa muppattāṟu tattuvak karuvikaḷum, oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy
   oṭuṅki, muṟaiyē tūyaveḷiyilum, aruḷilum, civattilumāka aṭaṅkutal,
   pēriṉpattai āṉmā peṟutaṟkē eṉpatu karuttu.
   cempil kaḷimpupōl, anāti toṭṭu āṉmāvil kalantu, poruḷ taṉmaiyāl oṉṟāki,
   palavakai āṟṟal paṭaittu, makattāṉa aṟivai maṟaittu, aniyāyam pala
   ceyyum āṇavam, taṉṉaik kāṭṭātu; talaivaṉaiyum kāṇac ceyvatillai. pāḻum
   iruḷāyp paṭarum mummalattuḷ oṉṟāṉa mūlamala āṟṟalai, aṟputa mutalvaṉ
   muṟaiyē aṭakkiṉaṉ.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   The Siddhantic Seers enumerate sixty external and thirty-six internal
   substances of our being. The outer tatvas are the movements of the
   physical vital planes of our being. Let us see how the inner mechanism
   functions.
   The internal tatvas are three-fold. They are grouped as Atma tatvas
   (24), Vidya Tatvas (7) and Siva tatvas (5) or five principles of
   Divinity.
   The meditative soul, in the state of equal and harmonious flow of
   energy, penetrates all the sheaths of body and reaches the state of
   emancipation as MeykandaThevar sings in this verse:
   maṇmutal nāḷam malarvittai; kalārūpam
       eṇṇiya īcar catācivamum naṇṇiṟ
       kalaiyuruvām nātamām cattiyataṉ kaṇṇām
       nilaiyatilām accivaṉtāḷ nēr.

   - civañāṉapōtam
   (oṉpatām nūṟpāvai viḷakkavanta mūṉṟām e.kā. ceyyuḷ)
   "maNmuthal nALam malarvidhdhai kalArUpam
   eNNiya Isar sathAsivamum naNNiR
   kalaiyuruvAm nAthamAm saththiyathan kaNNAm
   nilaiyathil Am ach -ivanthAL nEr"
   The stalk of this flower - maNmuthal nALam - indicates the 24 Atma
   tatvas starting from the gross level of Earth principle and their
   related subtle principles like Smell for Earth, Taste for Water, Form
   for Fire, Touch for Air and Sound for Ether. The 7 Vidya tatvas
   (conditional elements) and Suddha Vidya tatva (Pure gnosis) of Siva
   tatvas form the eitht petals. The other four principles of Divinity -
   Iswaram & Sathakiyam are the anthers; the Bindu or Sakthi and Nadham or
   Sivam are the ovary and ovules of that mystic flower. These ovules or
   seeds are also known as Mantras.
   Let me quote 'Yogi Shuddananda Bharathi' to clarify further:
   Begin quote:
   Paramasivam, the Supreme transcendent Reality, is pure Satchidananda.
   Sakthi or Consciousness is His personality. There is no existence for
   Sakthi without Sivam and there is no manifestation for Sivam without
   Sakthi; they are like the Sun and its rays.
   The duality of SivaSakthi is a polarity. The Supreme remains in the I
   am (Asmi of Vedanta) consciousness. From that bourgeons Aham, Idam (In
   this) consciousness. It is Unmani located in the pericarp of the
   Sahasrara.
   It is Unmani located in the pericarp of the Sahasrara. Samani below
   that is the sound priciple - Nadam - and verily at the beginning was
   the Word or Logos and from that the Idam or This-consciousness
   manifested. This is called Sadakya Tatvam. From this evolves the Iswara
   tatvam which is the state of self-gathered active consciousness in
   which 'I' subjectivises 'This'.
   Here begins Suddha Vidya Tatvam - the principle of pure cognition. The
   five forms of Maya are Time (kAlA), kalA (Limited power of doing),
   vidya (Limited Knowledge), Raga (attachment), Niyati (restraint). This
   along with Maya and Purusa, the soul, form the seven Vidya tatvas.
   From this breaks out the Atma Tatva, 24 in number. These are the
   Prakriti tatvas of Sankyas, the principles of the embodied soul: Five
   elements, Five sensations, Five senses of Knowing, Five of action and
   the Four inner instruments viz Mana, Buddhi, Citta, & Ahankara.
   These are the inner principles - 36 in number. The outer priciples are
   60, being the movements of the physical vital planes of our being;
   altogether, there are 96 principles, which have to be scrutinized,
   purified and transformed by the force of Maya. These are portions of
   our being, undiscovered by Physiology and Pathology.
   Siddhantha lays down the Sadhanas for their purification and
   perfection; the psychic force necessary for this transformation
   descends from the Mother-Grace of Sivam.
   Unquote
   As St.Meykandar sings, this has to start from bottom up - maNmuthal
   nALam or Muladhara to ManonmaNi.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   iruviṉai aṟuttal
   nalviṉai tīviṉaikaḷ eṉa viṉaikaḷ iraṇṭu. ivaikaḷiṉ payaṉ iṉpam tuṉpam.
   ivaikaḷai aṉupavikka, āṉmā piṟavi eṭukkum. mēlum kīḻumāṉa ulakaṅkaḷai
   aṭaiya iṟakkum.
   mīṭṭum vantu piṟakkum. viṉaiyiṉ payaṉāṉa uṭalum, iṉpamum tuṉpamum
   caṭam. avaikaḷ tāmē vantu āṉmāvai aṭaiyā. tēṭiya cottāṉa avaikaḷai
   aṉupavittut tirunta vēṇṭum; atu karutiya amalaṉ, avaikaḷai āṉmāvil
   cērppaṉ.
   vanta nōyai māṟṟa, maruntaḷikkum vaittiyaṉ pōl, kuṟṟam kaṇṭu taṇṭikkum
   kōvēntaṉ koḷkaipōl irukkiṉṟatu icceyal.
   āṉmā viṉaikaḷiṉ payaṉai aṉupavikkum; vittil maramum, marattil vittumpōl
   toṭarpu koṇṭu, mīṭṭum viṉaikaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. tiruvaruḷai virumpiya
   aṟivuṭaṉ, ceyal ceyvārkku ākāmiyam ēṟa vaḻiyillai.
   ñāṉikaḷaip pukaḻvārpāl nalviṉai ēṟum; ikaḻvārpāl tīviṉai ēṟum;
   cañcitamāka irunta tam iruviṉaikaḷaiyum iṅṅaṉam piṟarpāl utaṟiya piṉ,
   anta ñāṉikaḷtam cañcitam akalum. eṉiṉum, kaṇapatip perumāṉ taṉ
   karuṇaiyaik kāṭṭi, tam kaṭiya viṉaiyaik kaḻaṟṟutalaiyē virumpuvar
   ammēlōr. makattāṉa itu avartam maṉanilai.
   iruviṉai eṉpataṟku, valviṉai eṉap poruḷ koḷḷalumām. piṟappiṟku ētuvāka
   āṉmāviṉpāl amaintirukkum viṉai, eḷitil vilakkat takkatu aṉṟu. āṉma
   tavavaliyaiyum kaṭantu niṟkum āṟṟal uṭaiya atu, iruviṉaiyeṉap peyar
   peṟṟu irukkiṉṟatu.
   aṉaittum oṭuṅkutaṟku uriya kālam, makā caṅkāra kālam eṉap peṟum. ap
   pērūḻiyil, āṉmākkaḷ ellām kaṇapati aruḷāl kāraṇa māyaiyuḷ oṭuṅkum;
   avvamayam, avvāṉmākkaḷiṉ eñciya viṉaikaḷum, cūkkumamāyk kāraṇa māyaiyuḷ
   oṭuṅkum.
   kāraṇa māyaiyil oṭuṅkik kiṭappiṉum, mīṭṭum paṭaipput toṭaṅkuṅkāl,
   oṭuṅkiyirunta avviṉaikaḷē, uyirkaṭkut taṉu karaṇa puvaṉa pōkaṅkaṭku
   ētuvāka muḷaikkum; aṅṅaṉam ākātapaṭi, 'iruviṉai taṉṉai aṟuttu' eṉṟa
   nuṭpam,
   nuṉittu uṇarat takuvatu.
   'viṉaiyīṭṭap paṭuṅkāl, mantiram mutaliya attuvākkaḷiṉ iṭamāka,
   muṟaiyāṉē maṉam, vākku, kāyam eṉum mūṉṟāṉ īṭṭappaṭṭu, tūla kaṉmamāy
   ākāmiyam eṉap peyar peṟum. piṉṉarp pakkuvam āṅkāṟum cūkkuma kaṉmamāyp
   putti tattuvattiṉiṭamāka māyaiyiṟ kiṭakkum. ammāyai paṟṟukkōṭāka niṉṟu,
   puṉaruṟpavattil, taṉu karaṇam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku ētuvāy varumeṉak koḷka'
   eṉṟu civañāṉapōta iraṇṭām cūttira iraṇṭām atikaraṇattil, mātavac
   civañāṉa yōkikaḷ viḷakkam ceytal īṇṭu eṇṇat takum.
   aimperun toḻilkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa maṟaittal toḻilāl, uyirkaḷōṭu uṭaṉāki niṉṟu,
   viṉaippayaṉai nukarvikkiṉṟāṉ vimalaṉ.
   viṉaiyeccamē piṟappait tarukiṟatu. piṟanta piṉ, iṟappu tavirkka
   iyalātatu.
   'iṟappeṉum meymmaiyai yimmai yāvarkkum
   maṟappeṉum ataṉiṉmēṟ kēṭu maṟṟuṇṭō' eṉṟār kampar.
   paṟṟaṟṟu eytum vīṭupēṟu mika ariyatu. ataṉai viṭṭu, viṉaiyeccam
   uṭaiyōrē ulakil perumpālar. iṟappil viṉaiyeccam uḷatāyiṉ, uyirkku
   mīṇṭum piṟappu uṇṭu; ilatāyiṉ piṟavāmaiyum uḷatām. ivaikaḷai ellām
   eṇṇumpōtē, uyti karutiya uyir, pēṟu karuti, 'kaṇēcā kā! kaṇapatī
   eḷiyēṉaik kā!' eṉṟu kataṟuvatu iyaṟkaitāṉē! apperumāṉtāṉ, nam iruviṉai
   aṟuttu iruḷ kaṭital iyalum eṉṟu uṇarttum kālamē uytipeṟum kālam.
   'virinta cañcita viṉaikaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ nām
       viḻikka ventaṉa; viravum ippiṟappil
       poruntum valviṉai uṭaluṭaṉ akalum;
       punticēr aruḷ varuviṉai pōkkum'

   - eṉṟu maṇimoḻip perumāṉukkuc civam aṉṟu vāy malarntatu. iṉṟu
   auvaiyāriṉ viṉaiyai vīḻttik karumalattai vilakkiyatu kaṇapati eṉpatai,
   piṉvarum ōr aṭi evvaḷavu tiṟampaṭak kūṟukiṉṟatu! pāṭip pāruṅkaḷ! eṉṉa
   iṉpam!
   30. iruviṉai taṉṉai aṟuttiruḷ kaṭintu
   patavurai:
   iruviṉai taṉṉai - nalviṉai tīviṉaiyeṉum iruviṉaiyiṉ payaṉai,
   aṟuttu - muḻutum keṭuttu,
   iruḷ kaṭintu - (viṉaikaḷukku vaḻi ceyta āṇava) iruḷai akaṟṟi
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   iruviṉai nēroppil iṉṉaruṭ catti
       kuruveṉa vantu kuṇampala nīkkit
       tarumeṉu ñāṉattāl taṉceya laṟṟāl
       tirimalan tīrntu civaṉava ṉāmē.

   - tirumantiram (5-1527)
   'iraṭṭai viṉaikoṭu
   tiraṭṭu malavuṭal iṇakkamaṟa
   oru kaṇakkai aruḷvatum'
   eṉṟa tiruppukaḻ - 'peruttavacaṉa vakuppiṉ' aṭikku,
   aṭiyēṉ urai ivviṭam poruntak kāṇpōm:
   puṇṇiya pāvam iraṇṭuḷa pūmiyil
       naṇṇum poḻutaṟivār cila ñāṉikaḷ
       eṇṇi iraṇṭaiyum vēraṟuttu appuṟam
       aṇṇal iruppiṭam āyntu koḷvīrē.

   tantiram - 6 - 1647
   Jnana is Knowledge of Good and Evil:
   Good and evil, they are two in this world
   As they seek God, some Jnanis know them;
   As you cognize them two and uproot them
   Then shall you perceive Lord's Abode Beyond.
   The Seers realize that God is beyong good and evil,
   beyond time and space.
   nākam uṭaluri pōlunal aṇṭaca
   māka naṉāvil kaṉā maṟantallatu
   pōkalum ākum araṉaruḷālē ceṉ
   ṟēkum ṭamceṉṟu rupayaṉ uṇṇumē.
   tantiram - 8 - 2132
   In the Waking State Dreams are Forgotten;
   So it is Through Successive Lives:
   Even as the snake sloughs off its skin
   And another assumes;
   Even as the bird its shell leaves
   And another life pursues;
   In its waking state the Jiva forgets
   Happenings of the dream state;
   Thus does Jiva from one body to another migrate;
   Until with Grace of Hara
   It reaches where it is destined to be;
   And there experiences
   The Karmas two, good and evil.
   uṇṭu naraka cuvarkkattil uḷḷaṉa
   kaṇṭu viṭuñcūkkaṅ kāraṇamāc celap
   paṇṭu toṭarap parakāya yōki pōl
   piṇṭam eṭukkum piṟappu iṟappu eytiyē.
   tantiram - 8 - 2133
   Cycle of Births and Deaths:
       Having experienced hell and heaven,
       Jiva leaves Subtle body;
       Entering Causal body its course continues;
       And unto the Yogi that transmigrates
       Enters yet another body;
       Thus entangled in cycle of birth and death.
       tāṉ avaṉākiya taṟparam tāṅkiṉōṉ
       āṉavai māṟṟip paramattu aṭaintiṭum
       ēṉai uyirviṉaikku eytum iṭamceṉṟum
       vāṉum nilaṉum pukuntum varuntumē.
       tantiram - 8 - 2133
       The Birth Cycle ends only when Jiva Unites in God:
       The Jiva that realized
       "I" and "You" are one,
       Is in Tatpara state;
       Its course inherent diverting
       Will reach Param;
       The rest of Jivas
       Reaching their destined abodes
       In heaven and earth
       Will in sorrow wallow.
       iruviṉaiyoppu eṉum nilai
       cērntē piṉ nirvāṇam.
       iruviṉai ottiṭa iṉṉaruḷ catti
       maruviṭa ñāṉattil ātaṉam maṉṉik
       kuruviṉaik koṇṭaruḷ cattimuṉ kūṭṭip
       perumalam nīṅkip piṟavāmai cuttamē.

   tantiram - 8 - 2262
   Sakala Jiva'a Journey to Suddha State
       They reach the State
       When deeds good and bad
       Equable become (Iruvinai Oppu);
       When Sakti's Grace
       On them descends (Sathinipadam);
       When on the pedestal of Grace
       They thus get seated,
       Then with the aid of Guru Holy
       They reach the Presence of Sakti's Grace;
       Finally rid of the Primordial Mala (Anava)
       They reach the State of Suddha
       That no birth thereafter gives.
       taṉṉaiyaṟiyātu uṭalai muṉ tāṉeṉṟāṉ
       taṉṉaimuṉ kaṇṭāṉ turiyantaṉaik kaṇṭāṉ
       uṉṉum turiyamum īcaṉōṭu oṉṟākkāl
       piṉṉaiyum vantu piṟantiṭun tāṉē.

   tantiram - 8 - 2264
   Self-Realization Only in Turiya State--It Is Not Ultimate:
       Knowing not the Self,
       Jiva deemed body as the Self;
       When he saw the Real Self;
       He attained Turiya State;
       But even in that Turiya State,
       If he, Jiva, with Lord united not,
       He will again born be, here below.
       cākkiran taṉṉil atītan talaippaṭil
       ākkiya vanta vayintava māṉanta
       nōkkum piṟappaṟu nōṉmutti cittiyām
       vākkum maṉamum maruval ceyyāvē.

   tantiram - 8 - 2265
   Only With Turiyatita State Birth Cycle Ends
       If in Waking State
       The Jiva realizes the Atita State
       The Vaindavas (the Tattvas) that Maya caused
       Will their malevolence shed;
       The birth's whirl will cease;
       Goodly Mukti and Siddhi then attained;
       Speech and thought cease to be.

   lalāṭam eṉum puruvamattiyil avastai aṉaittaiyum aṭakkavalla ñāṉiyarkkē
   'iraṭṭai viṉaikoṭu tiraṭṭu malavuṭal iṇakkamaṟa oru kaṇakkai aruḷvatum'
   nikaḻum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   nāṉku vaḻikaḷil nāṟpayaṉ
   nampaṉ aruḷnilaiyai nampa vēṇṭum; uṇarvil namputal ūṉṟa vēṇṭum. aṭaiya
   vēṇṭum nam amalaṉai eṉṉum ārvak kaṉal atikarikka vēṇṭum. āṟṟāmai mika
   vēṇṭum.
   āṉaimukap paramaṉai aṭaiya, nalañciṟanta vaḻikaḷ nāṉku. paṇivaḻi,
   maintaṉ nilai, naṭpu neṟi, kātal mārkkam eṉa avaikaḷai aṟivittuḷar
   aṟavōr.
   mētaku kōyilil meḻukkiṭal, malar koytal, ariya mālai toṭuttu aṇital,
   paraṉaip pāṭal, toṇṭarait toḻutal, toṇṭu ceytal mutaliyaṉa paṇivaḻi
   eṉappeṟum. inneṟiyil payilpavar, vittakak kaṇapati ulakil vīṟṟirukkap
   peṟuvar. itu cālōkapatavi eṉappeṟum.
   nāṇmalar, naṟumpukai, tiruviḷakku, tirumañcaṉa nīr mutaliya vaḻipaṭu
   poruḷkaḷait tēṭip peṟutal; aivakait tūymai ceytal, iṟaivarukku iṭam
   uruvam mutaliyaṉa amaittal; aṉpiṉāl vaḻipaṭal; aḻal ōmputal mutaliyaṉa
   maintaṉ nilai. payaṉāṉa inneṟiyiṟ payilpavar, parama kaṇapatiyiṉ
   aṇmaiyil irukkum patam aṭaivar. itu cāmīpyam ākum.
   poṟikaḷaip pulaṉvaḻi pōkāmal taṭuttal; iruvakai mūccaiyum aṭakkal;
   ātāra muṟaiyai aṟital; aṅkaṅku vāḻum kaṭavuḷarai vaḻipaṭal; ucci
   naṭuvil cērtal; aṅkuḷa kamala arumpai malarvittal; mati maṇṭalattai
   iḷakac ceytal; ataṉiṭattu uḷḷa amutai uṭal muḻuvatum paravac ceytal;
   pēroḷip piḻampām vināyaka uruvai nayamuṟa eṇṇiyiruttal; ivai naṭpuneṟi
   allatu cārūpa patavi eṉap peṟum. uyarnta inneṟiyai mēṟkoḷvōr ōṅkāra
   kaṇapatiyiṉ uruvam aṭaivar. paṇivaḻi, maintaṉ nilai, naṭpu neṟi
   mūṉṟāṉum aṭaiyum naṟpēṟu patamutti eṉṟu peṟum.
   nūlkaḷiṉ koḷkaiyai āyntu uṇarvar, ulaku, uyir, iṟaiyeṉum mūṉṟai
   uṇarttavalla civañāṉattaiyē cintippar. kāṇpāṉ, kāṭci, kāṇappaṭu poruḷ
   mūṉṟum tōṉṟātu pōka, kaṭṭaṟṟa kaṭavuḷil kalantu niṟpar; ituvē kātal
   mārkkam. ivarkaḷ peṟuvatu cāyucciyam eṉṟa paramutti.
   maṟṟoru vakai
   muppattāṟu tattuvaṅkaḷaiyum niyati kaḷaivatu paṇivaḻi; ataṉpiṉ, taṉṉai
   aruḷāl aṟintu, avaṟṟiṉ muṭivil niṟpatu cālōka patam.
   āṅkut taṉakku aṟivitta aruḷait taricittu, ataṉpiṉ aruḷ eṉavum, tāṉ
   eṉavum pirittup pārātu, taṟpōtattai aṭakkuvatu puttiravaḻi; aruḷē
   vaṭivāṉa ataṉ payaṉ, cāmīpa patam.
   aṅṅaṉam amarntu, taṟpōtam aṇuvaḷavum tōṉṟāmal oḻippatu naṭpu vaḻi;
   paraiyē tāṉ āṉa ataṉ payaṉ, cārūpa patam.
   paraiyum vilaka utikkum parama āṉantam, kātal vaḻiyeṉap peṟum. anta
   iṉpattiṉ atītam āvatu cāyucciya patam. ñāṉa nūlkaḷ, ariya nuṭpam pala
   tōṉṟa ivaikaḷai aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
   aruḷārnta āṉaimukap paramaṉ, innāṉku vaḻikaḷaiyum, ataṉ payaṉaiyum
   aruḷiṉaṉ eṉkiṟatu piṉvarum ōr aṭi.
   31. talamoru nāṉkum tanteṉakkaruḷi
   patavurai:
   talam oru nāṉkum - (cariyai, kiriyai, yōkam, ñāṉam eṉum
   vaḻikaḷāl varum) oppaṟṟa (cālōkam, cāmīpam, cārūpam,
   cāyucciyam eṉum) nāṉku iṭaṅkaḷaiyum,
   tantu eṉakku aruḷi - aṭiyēṉukku iṉitu kāṭṭiyaruḷi (eṉṟavāṟu).
   cariyaiyāṉa uru; kiriyaiyāṉa aruvuru; yōkamāṉa aru; ñāṉamāṉa uḷḷakkaṇ
   eṉum nāṉku iṭaṅkaḷaiyum, peṟaṟku ariya aṭi ñāṉattaiyum aṭiyēṉ peṟa
   vaittu eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷa iṅku iṭamuṇṭu.
   'virumpum cariyai mutal meyññāṉam nāṉkum
   arumpu malar kāykaṉi pōl ākum parāparamē'
   eṉpar tāyumāṉār.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   tirumantiram aintām tantirattil (pāṭal 1443 - 1513) patamutti,
   paramuttiyāṉa nāṉku vaḻikaḷ naṉku viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   cārnta meyñ ñāṉattōr tāṉava ṉāyiṉōr
       cērntaveṇ yōkattar cittar camātiyōr
       āynta kiriyaiyōr aruccaṉai tappātōr
       tērnta cariyaiyōr nīḷnilat tōrē.

   cālōkam, cāmīpyam, cārūpam eṉpavai iṭṭuc cellum patamukti kaṭanta,
   cāyujyam eṉṟa paramuktikkum aṭippaṭai 'yāṉ eṉatu' eṉṟa nilai kaṭattal
   maṭṭumē.
   cāyucciya nilaiyē tuṟavaṟamāvatu. ateṉṉa patamukti maṟṟum paramukti?
   paramporuḷāṉatu, piṟantiṟantōyntirukkum uyirkaḷiṉ mēl karuṇai koṇṭu
   avaṟṟait taṉ tiruvaṭiyuṭaṉ oṉṟākkik koḷvatu aṟakkaruṇaiyeṉil,
   viṭṭuvilaki vīṭupēṟu maṭṭumē kuṟiyeṉak koḷḷāmal maṟṟīṇṭu varum
   uyirkaḷiṉ mēl koḷvatu maṟakkaruṇaiyākum.
   cila uyirkaḷ cilakālam tiruvaṭipēṟṟil tiḷaittum tattam viṉaip
   pucippiṉpaṭi
   mīṇṭum varum. mīṇṭuvārā mukti paramuktiyeṉil, avaciyam varukaiyil
   appaṭi mīṇṭum varum nilaikku patamuktiyeṉṟu colvar.
   cālōkam - īcaṉavaṉ kūṭṭattil avvulakil vāḻvatu
   cāmīpam - īcaṉ camīpattil avaṉ āṭumpātamaṭi puṉitavatiyār pōl vāḻum
   pēṟu
   cārūpam - īcaṉiṉ vaṭivē tāmumeyti vāḻvatu
   cāyucciyam - īcaṉē taṉṉuḷ kalantu tāṉ avaṉākiviṭṭa attuvita nilai.
   cārūpam varai pēṟupeṟṟa uyirkaḷ mīṇṭum iṟaṅkivaruvatu collak kēṭkiṟōm.
   iraṇṭaṟak kalantuviṭal paramukti. avvaḷavutāṉ.
   muktikkēṟpa piripavai mārkkaṅkaḷ eṉpar:
   avai muṟaiyē:
   tācamārkkam - eṉ kaṭaṉ paṇiceytu kiṭattalē eṉṟiruttal.
   catputramārkkam - āṇai namatē eṉṟu vēl tūkkum urimai!
   kavaṉikka:avvaḻi piṟantōrellām catputtirarē.
   cakamārkkam - itu namma āḷu eṉṟa pāvaṉaiyitu. vaṉṟoṇṭar vaḻi.
   ñāṉamārkkam - aravintar colvatu pōl lēcar pārvai koṇṭavarivar.
   muṭintamuṭivāy mūṭṭai kaṭṭik koṇṭu kāttiruppavar.
   caivak kuravar nālvaraiyum mēṟcoṉṉa mārkkarītiyilāṉa aṇukumuṟaiyil
   aṭakkuvateṉpatu iyalāteṉiṉum, paṇṭu toṭṭu varum orupuritalukkākac
   cuṭṭukiṟēṉ. atuvum avarkaḷē tām poḻinta niṟaimoḻikaḷāṉa maṟaimoḻikaḷūṭē
   kuṟippāl uṇarttuvatiliruntu:
   eṉkaṭaṉ paṇiceytu kiṭappatē eṉpatum, puḻuvāyp piṟakkiṉum puṇṇiyā uṉṉaṭi
   eṉ maṉattē vaḻuvātirukka varamtara vēṇṭum eṉpatum apparperumāṉatu
   viṇṇappam. atu tācamārkkattiṉ uṉṉatattaic cuṭṭa vantatu. cariyai eṉṟum
   colvar. itu cālōka mukti.
   'vētavēḷviyai nintaṉai ceytuḻal' kūṭṭam kaṇṭu poṅkiya tirumakaṉār
   pālaṟāvāyarāṉa emperumāṉ campantar catputtiramārkkam cuṭṭupavar.
   kiriyai eṉṟum colvar. ulakiyal cuḻaṟci pātikkum vakaiyil acuracaktikaḷ
   mikum pōtellām vēleṭuttu iraṅkivarum, iṟaṅkivarum kuḻantaiyatu. itu
   cāmīpya mukti. kuḻantai takappaṉukkeṉṟum aṇmaiyil allavā irukkum!
   'ivaralātu illaiyō pirāṉār? aṭa pōyyā' eṉṟu īcaṉaiyē snēkitta, avaraiyē
   kātaliyiṭam tūtaṉuppiya cuntarar cuṭṭuvatu yōka mārkkameṉum
   cakamārkkam. itu cārūpam. ālālacuntarar eṉṟu āṭiyil tōṉṟiya īcaṉiṉ
   vaṭivē avar.
   māṇikkavācakar ñāṉamārkkam. atuvē caṉmārkkam. ellāp piṟappum
   piṟantiḷaittu, 'meyyē uṉ poṉṉaṭikaḷ kaṇṭu iṉṟu vīṭuṟṟēṉ' eṉṟu
   muṭintamuṭipāy mūṭṭai kaṭṭi viṭṭa maṇiyatu. 'teruppāṭal uvanteṉaiyum
   civamākkum teyvam' eṉṟu caṉmārkkavaḻiyē karaintupōy viṭṭa vaḷḷalāraiyum
   cuṭṭalām. cīvaṉ kuṟukic civamākum inta maṟṟīṇṭuvārā iṟuti nilaiyē
   cāyucciyamākum paramuktiyākum.
   cāyuc ciyañcāk kirātītañ cārutal
       cāyuc ciyamupa cāntattut taṅkutaṟ
       cāyuc ciyañciva mātal muṭivilāc
       cāyuc ciyamaṉat tāṉanta cattiyē!

   tirumantiram - 1513
   Sayujya is the state of Jagra-Atita, the Beyond-consciousness;
       Sayujya is to abide for ever in Upasantha, the peace that passes
       all
       understanding;
       Sayujya is to become Siva himself;
       Sayujya is to experience the infinite power of inward bliss,
       Forever and ever!

   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   māyai nivāraṇam
   tūyatu, tūymaiyillātatu, mūlappakuti eṉa māyai mūṉṟu eṉalai muṉ kaṇṭōm.
   mutirnta ulakelām tōṉṟutaṟku, ivaikaḷē mutal kāraṇam. kaṇapatip perumāṉ
   kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi, tūya māyaiyiṉ kīḻ, tūyatallā māyai tōṉṟum; mūlappakuti,
   ataṉiliruntu muḷaikkum.
   māyai eṉṟum maṉṉiyuḷatu. atu aruvam. poruḷ taṉmaiyāl oṉṟu. ulakiṟku
   vittu. paṭarntu eṅkum paraviyatu. iṟaivaṉatu caṭacakti. uṭal
   mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mutal kāraṇam. āṉmāviṉ kaṭṭu, mey iyalpiṟku māṟāṉa
   uṇarvait tarutaliṉ,
   mayakkamum ceyyum. mayakkātatu tūya māyai. uṇarat takka icceyti, eṉṟum
   nam
   maṉattil iruttaṟku uriyatu.
   ivaikaḷai ūṉṟi uṇara, uḷpula nuṭpam pala veḷippaṭum.
   32. malamoru mūṉṟiṉ mayakkam aṟuttē
   patavurai:
   malam oru mūṉṟiṉ - (āṇavam, kaṉmam, māyai ākiya) mummalattuḷ oṉṟāṉa,
   mayakkam aṟuttē - (māyāmalattiṉ) mayakkai (aṭiyoṭu) akaṟṟi (eṉṟavāṟu).
   patiṉeṭṭām aṭiyil, māyātu perukum piṟaviyil irunta mayakkattai aṟuttāṉ
   eṉṟār. muppatām aṭiyil iruviṉai viṭuttāṉ; iruḷaik keṭuttāṉ eṉṟār.
   iṅku, mummalattuḷ oṉṟāṉa māyā malattil āṉmā mayaṅkutalai, aṭiyōṭu
   tolaittāṉ eṉṉum arumai eṇṇaṟku uriyatu.
   piñcāy iruntapōtu, ōṭum vittum piripaṭātu iruntaṉa; paḻamāṉa nilaiyil,
   ōṭum vittum piripaṭum. atupōl, aṟivu mutirāp paruvattil piripaṭātu
   iruntatu
   mummalam. aṟivu mutirnta amayam, puṟamiruntu pōrkkum māyai kaṉmaṅkaḷum,
   āṉmāvil maruviya muḷaiyāṉa āṇavamum, anniyamāy nīṅkum; ākāmiyamum
   aḻiveytum.
   kātaṟṟa ūciyāyiṉ, nūliruntum payaṉillai; atupōl, taṟpōtam tavirntārkku,
   toṭarpu etaṉālum tollai illai; avarkaṭku, ācai nācamē āṉantam.
   itu iṅkuk karutaṟku uriyatu.
   'vaṇaṅki niṟpōrkkaruḷ utavum emperumāṉai, evvuyirkaḷum koṭu māmalam
   oruvi yaṉpuru vāki yaṭaṅkavaittu āṭkoḷum ukaḷa paṅkaya pātaṉai'
   - eṉum vināyakar piḷḷaittamiḻ, iṅku nam niṉaiviṟ pukuntu
   niḻaliṭukiṉṟatu allavā?
   katavaṭaippu
   kuṟaivilā niṟaivāṉa aṟivāṉāṉ amalaṉ. kuṟaiyē niṟainta aṟivuṭaiyatu
   āṉmā. aṭiyōṭu kuṟaikaḷai akaṟṟal vēṇṭum. ataṟkeṉṟē, uṭal karaṇa puvaṉa
   pōkaṅkaḷai, uyirkaṭkup perumāṉ utaviyuḷaṉ.
   utavi kiṭaitta piṉ, piḻaikaḷai akaṟṟuvataṟku āṉa aṉupava aṟivai aṭaiya,
   ulakil uyirkkiṟatu uyir. ataṟkeṉṟē uṭalai - karaṇaṅkaḷai ūkkukiṉṟatu;
   taṉ iccai, aṟivu, ceyalkaḷai eḻuppukiṉṟatu; ariya ceyalaip puṟavulakil
   āṟṟukiṉṟatu. aṉupavam viḷaiya, puṟacceyal maṭṭum pōtuvatu illai.
   puṟavulaka pōkattait tuyttaṟkum, avaikaḷai ākkaiyiṉ akattil cērttaṟkum,
   aimpulak katavukaḷ vāyilāka amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉma uṇarvu puṟam
   pōkātapaṭi, akkatavukaḷai aṭaittu viṭuvatē arumaiyāṉa vaḻi.
   oṉpatu vāyil
   kaṇ etiril paṭukiṉṟaṉa, pōka aṉupavak kaḻivukaḷ; avaikaḷ veḷippaṭaṟku
   uriya vaḻikaḷ oṉpatu. ivaikaḷai, oṉpatu vāyil eṉṟu ōtuvar. navattuvāram
   eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi marapu.
   kāmikkuk kuṟivaḻi kīḻiṟaṅki virayamākum vintu, aimpulak katavai
   aṭaikkum aṟivuṭaiyōrkku, civacaktik kaṉalāl mēlēṟum; makimaiyuḷa
   cuḻumuṉai vaḻiyē pōy, makattāṉa amutamāka māṟum.
   itaṟkeṉṟu ōreḻuttu mantirattai uruvēṟṟuvar. pirāṇa āyāmam ceyvar.
   itaṉāl, ulaka pōkam tuykkum maṉam oṭuṅkum; oṉpatu vāyilum, aimpulak
   katavukaḷum tāmē aṭaipaṭum.
   uyarnta innilaiyai eytiṉār uṭaluḷ, ñāṉa ākāya oḷi viḷaṅkum; aruḷ aṟivu
   tittikkum; civapōkam cittikkum. eṉṉa aṟputamāṉa ceyti! āṉaimukap
   paramaṉ ivaikaḷaiyum tamakku aṟivuṟuttiṉaṉ eṉkiṟār, namatu auvaiyār.
   33. oṉpatu vāyil oru mantirattāl
   34. aimpulak katavai aṭaippatum kāṭṭi
   patavurai:
   oṉpatu vāyil - (irukaṇ, irucevi, nāciyiṉ irutuḷai, vāy, eruvāy, karuvāy
   eṉum) oṉpatu (uṟuppukaḷiṉ) vaḻikaḷaiyum,
   aimpulak katavai - aimpoṟikaḷāṉa katavukaḷaiyum,
   oru mantirattāl - oppaṟṟa (ōṅkāra) mantirattāl,
   aṭaippatum kāṭṭi - (puruṭaṉāṉa āṉmā veḷiyē pōkātapaṭi) aṭaippataiyum
   kāṇpittu (eṉṟavāṟu).
   nīṭittu uṭalai niṟutti vaikka muṭiyātu; uriya vāyil, ataṟku oṉpatu
   uḷḷaṉa allavā! ataṉ vaḻi uyirō orunāḷ ōṭiviṭum.
   aimpulak katavukaḷai aṭikkaṭi tiṟakka, oṉpatu vāyililum pōkkuvaraviṟku
   iṭam piṟakkiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam ākātapaṭi, uṟuti uraitta muṟai itu.
   27, 28 - m aṭikaḷil 'aimpulaṉ taṉṉai aṭakkum upāyam iṉpuṟu karuṇaiyiṉ
   iṉiteṉakkaruḷi' eṉṟār. a·tu, uriya pulaṉai aṭakkum upāyam; iṅkuk
   kūṟiyatu, poṟi pulaṉkaḷaic ceyalaṟṟaṉavākac ceyyum ceyti. aṭakkum
   upāyam, ñāṉacaktiyāl amaiyum; aṭaikkum ceyti, kiriyācaktiyāl āvatu.
   inta vēṟṟumaikaḷ iṅku niṉaikkat takuvaṉa. eṉṉa aṟputamāṉa aṉupavam!
   'poḷḷal ivvuṭalaip poruḷeṉṟu
       poruḷum cuṟṟamum pōkamum āki
       meḷḷa niṉṟavar ceyvaṉa ellām
       vārāmē tavirkkum vitiyāṉai'

   - eṉum cuntarar tēvāramum,
   'ōṅkārat(tu) uḷḷoḷi yuḷḷē utayamuṟ(ṟu)
       āṅkāram aṟṟa aṉupavam kaikūṭār
       cāṅkālam uṉṉār piṟavāmai cārvuṟār
       nīṅkāc camayattuḷ niṉṟoḻin tārkaḷē'
       - eṉak kaviṉum tirumantiramum iṅkuk karutat takum.

   ātāra nilai
   yōkam iruvakai. avai ātāram, nirātāram eṉap peṟum. inta iru nilaiyum,
   civakaṇapati tiruvaṭiyil āṉmāvaic cērppikkum. kutam, kuṟikaḷiṉ iṭaiyil
   mūlātāram uḷatu. etaṟkum mūlar eṉpataṟku ēṟpa, im mūlanilattil kaṇapati
   irukkiṉṟār. payaṉāṉa itaṉai iṉṉum pāruṅkaḷ!
   kuṟimūlattil cuvātiṭṭāṉam; iṅkup piramatēvar irukkiṉṟār. untiyil
   maṇipūrakam uḷatu; teyvam itaṟkut tirumāl.
   itayattil anākatam irukkiṟatu; uruttirar iṅkut talaivarāka uḷar.
   vittakak kaṇṭam vicutti eṉap peṟum; iṅku makēcar irukkiṉṟār. ākñai,
   puruvanaṭuvil amaintu irukkiṉṟatu. cārum inta iṭattiṉ talaivar
   catācivar.
   āṟākiya inta ātāraṅkaḷ, vīṇāttaṇṭiṉ aṭiyiliruntu naṭṭuvaitta aṅkucam
   pōl, puruvanaṭuvām cuḻumuṉaivarai nīṭik kūṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
   ātāraṅkaḷil irukkum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷai, niyatippaṭi aḻunta niṉaikka
   niṉaikka, annuṇṇiya vaṭivukaḷil, muṟaiyē āṉma uṇarvu kalantu karakkum.
   annilaiyil niṉaivāṉum, niṉaiporuḷum, niṉaivum nīṅkum. iṉiya itu, ātāra
   yōkam eṉap peṟum.
   innilai kaivantapiṉ, kaṟpaṉai kaṭantavaṉ, kalantu etaṉilum kalavātāṉ,
   akaṇṭaṉ eṉum anta aruḷ caktiyāḷaṉai, uḷḷak kaṇkoṇṭu uṇara varum. ataṉ
   piṉ, 'avaṉ vēṟu nāṉ vēṟu' eṉum niṉaivu nīṅkum; muttikku uriya mōṉam
   cittikkum. itayattil iṟainilai, nilaipēṟāka niṟkum. itu nirātāra yōkam
   eṉappeṟum.
   aṅkucattāl yāṉaiyai aṭakkum pākaṉaippōl, pirāṇaṉai iṭai piṅkalai
   vaḻi virayam ceyyāmal, āṟu ātāra vaḻiyē cellumāṟu aṭakkukiṉṟaṉar
   yōkiyar. uḷḷanupava inta uṇmaiyaik kaṇapati ōtukiṉṟār. ōtiya arumaiyai,
   piṉvarum aṭikaḷil peritu kāṇkiṟōm.
   35. āṟātārattu aṅkuca nilaiyum
   36. pēṟā niṟuttip pēccurai aṟuttē
   patavurai:
   āṟu ātārattu - āṟu ātāraṅkaḷil,
   aṅkuca nilaiyum - aṅkucam pōṉṟa nilaiyaiyum,
   pēṟā niṟutti - nilai peyarāmal niṟkac ceytu,
   pēccu urai aṟuttu - pēcum pēccai viṭacceytu (mauṉiyākki)
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   vāḻkkaiyil ūṭuruvi varum iṉpattil uruḷār, tuṉpattil tuvaḷār,
   ivviraṇṭaiyum oppa eṇṇuvar; ataṉ mūlam aruḷaṟivu peṟuvar; tērnta
   itaṉāl tirikaraṇa mavuṉam tērvar; i·tē vīṭupēṟṟiṟku vēṇṭap peṟum.
   'mōṉam eṉpatu ñāṉa varampu' eṉpatu mutumoḻi.
   ‘cēvikkum mantiram cellum ticaipeṟa
   āvikkuḷ mantiram ātāram āvaṉa
   pūvukkuḷ mantiram pōkkaṟa nōkkiṭil
   āvikkuḷ mantiram aṅkucam āmē'
   - eṉa aṅkucam kuṟittut tirumūlar kuṟippiṭum aṉupavam iṅku āytaṟku
   uriyatu.
   iṭaipiṅkalaiyiṉ eḻuttaṟivittal
   mūṉṟu kalai
   cūriyaṉ, cantiraṉ, akkiṉi eṉṉum nāṭikaḷ mūṉṟu uḷḷē ōṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   avaikaḷai piṅkalai, iṭaikalai, cuḻumuṉai eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iṭaikalai
   eṉpatu iṭatu nāciyil; piṅkalai valatu nāciyil; ippaṭi iru nāciyilum
   pōkkuvaravu ceyyum cuvācam. iṭakalai uṭaliṉ iṭappākattum, piṅkalai
   tēkattiṉ valappākattum iruppatu pōl, cuḻumuṉai inta uṭaliṉ naṭuvil
   irukkiṟatu.
   pirāṇaṉ acaivē citta acaivu. ataṉāl pirāṇaṉait tam vayappaṭuttal
   takkatu. pirāṇāyāmattil veḷivāyuvai uṭkoḷḷal pūrakam; uṭkoṇṭatai
   uḷniṟuttuvatu kumpakam; kumpittatai veḷiviṭuvatu irēcakam eṉappeṟum.
   piṅkalaikku uriya eḻuttu akaram; iṭakalaikku uriyatu ukaram; akkiṉi
   kalaikkuriyatu makaram. cirattil uḷatu āyira itaḻk kamalam. ariya
   eḻuttukkaḷai, muṟaiyē kurumukamāy aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. vaḻi muṟai
   terintāl, kapāla vāyilum viḷaṅkum.
   iṭaikalai, piṅkalai iṭaiyaṟātu nikaḻtaliṉ, uṭalil niṟkiṟatu uyir.
   'auvaiyē! nī anta iruvaḻi ilaṅkum pirāṇavāyuvai niṟuttu. avaikaḷaic
   cuḻumuṉai vaḻiyē celuttu! ucci ōṭṭil ñāṉaveḷi uḷatu; uyarnta ataṉoṭu
   uṟavāṭal cey!' eṉṟu upatēcam ceytāṉām perumāṉ. eṉṉa āṉantam!
   37. iṭaipiṅ kalaiyiṉ eḻuttaṟivittuk
   38. kaṭaiyiṟ cuḻumuṉaik kapālamum kāṭṭi
   patavurai:
   iṭai - iṭaināṭikkuriya ōreḻuttu mantirattaiyum,
   piṅkalaiyiṉ eḻuttaṟivittu - piṅkalai nāṭikkuriya mantirattaiyum (aiyam
   tiripaṟa)
   aṟivittaruḷi,
   cuḻumuṉaik kaṭaiyil - akkiṉi kalaiyāṉa cuḻumuṉai nāṭiyiṉ muṭivil,
   kapālamum kāṭṭi - (kāṇattakka) piramarantirattaiyum kāṇpittaruḷi
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   'ūṉil uyirppai oṭukki oṇcuṭar
   ñāṉa viḷakkiṉai yēṟṟi naṉpulattu
   ēṉai vaḻitiṟan(tu) ēttuvārk(ku) iṭar
   āṉa keṭuppaṉa añce ḻuttumē'
   - eṉum ñāṉacampantar tēvāram iṅku niṉaiviṟku varukiṟatu allavā!
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   ellāk kalaiyum iṭaipiṅ kalainaṭuc
   collā naṭunāṭi yūṭē toṭarmūlañ
   cellā eḻuppic cirattuṭaṉ cērtalāl
   nallōr tiruvaṭi naṇṇi niṟpōrē.
   Rouse All Kalas and Reach God
   All Kalas from the Left and Right Nadis
   Pass through the Central Nadi;
   Kindle the Kundalini Fire in Muladhara;
   They reach the Cranium at top,
   To pay homage at the Feet of the Great One.
   (Tirumantiram - Verse 3-857 - Chandra Yoga)
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   pēcā mantiram
   payaṉāṉa māyaiyil miḷirkiṉṟatu parikraka cakti. pāmpuruvam paṭaittu,
   mūlātārattai iṭam koṇṭu, tūṅkum nilaiyil toṅkukiṉṟatu itu. yōkiyar
   iṭattil maṭṭum, viḻitteḻuntu mēl nōkkum. itaṟkuk kuṇṭaliṉi eṉavum
   peyaruṇṭu. ariya yōkikaḷ itaṉai aṟivar.
   mūlātāramum, cuvātiṭṭāṉamum, akkiṉi maṇṭalam; maṇipūrakamum, aṉākatamum
   cūriya maṇṭalam; vicuttiyum, ākkiṉaiyum cantira maṇṭalam eṉap peṟum.
   iṅṅaṉam āṟu ātāraṅkaḷ, iraṇṭu iraṇṭāyp piṇaintu irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta mūṉṟu
   maṇṭalattiṉum ūṭuruvic cellum tūṇpōl, kulavit toṅki eḻukiṟatu
   kuṇṭaliṉi.
   pirāṇavāyu kumpakam āka, mūlak kaṉal mūṇṭeḻum. takataka eṉum kaṉal
   vemmaikkut takkapaṭi, mēṉmaik kuṇṭaliṉi mēl nōkkum. avvaḷavutāṉā?
   mantira nuṭpattai nāvāl eḻuti, avaikaḷai maṉam uṇara vaḻi ceyyum; ariya
   icceytiyai piṉ varum aṭikaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
   39. mūṉṟu maṇṭalattiṉ muṭṭiya tūṇiṉ
   40. nāṉṟeḻu pāmpiṉ nāvil uṇartti
   patavurai:
   mūṉṟu maṇṭalattiṉ - mati, pariti, tī eṉum mūṉṟu maṇṭalaṅkaḷiṉum,
   muṭṭiya tūṇiṉ - muṭṭic cellum vīṇāt taṇṭam pōl,
   nāṉṟu eḻu pāmpiṉ - toṅki niṟkum pāmpuruvak kuṇṭaliṉiyiṉ,
   nāvil uṇartti - (uraikkat takkaṉavaṟṟai) nāvāl uṇarac ceytu (eṉṟavāṟu).
   'mūla nilattil atōmukamāy
       mukiḻttu viḻiyiṉ poṭutuyilum
       mūrip pāmpaik kāl aṉalai
       mūṭṭi yeḻuppi nilamāṟum
       cīla moṭumpōyt taricittuc
       ceḻumā matiyiṉ amutakaṭal
       tēkkit tiḷaittaṅ(ku) acaivaṟumōr
       teyvat tavattōr uḷaviḻiyil
       kālum tīpam miṉalpantam
       kaṭikūr viricu katirmatipōl
       kāṭṭum oḷiyāy, ivaiyāvum
       kaṭantāṅku aḻivil pēroḷiyāy
       cāla viḷaṅkum vintuveṉum
       taṭamā mayilōy tālēlō
       tamarak kaṭalaik kaṭaintamutam
       taruvōṉ marukā tālēlō'

   - eṉṟu citampara aṭikaḷ kūṟum cannitimuṟaik kuṟippu, iṅkuk kuṇikkat
   takum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   mūṉṟu maṇṭalattiṉum muṭṭiniṉṟa tūṇilum
       nāṉṟa pāmpiṉ vāyilum naviṉṟeḻunta aṭcaram
       īṉṟatāyum appaṉum eṭutturaitta mantiram
       tōṉṟumōr eḻuttuḷē colla eṅkum illaiyē!

   - civavākkiyar - aṟivunilai - 96
   eruviṭum vācaṟ kiruviraṉ mēlē
       karuviṭum vācaṟ kiruviraṟ kīḻē
       uruviṭuñ cōtiyai uḷkaval lārkkuk
       karuviṭuñ cōti kalantu niṉṟāṉē.
       - tirumantiram - 3-584, pirattiyākāram

   Kundalini Yoga Ends Birth
   Two finger length above the anus
   Two finger length below the sex organ,
   Lies the Kundalini Fire
   If you can meditate on the light
   That burns there,
   You shall be One with Lord,
   Who all births ends.
   koṇṭa virataṅ kuṟaiyāmaṟ ṟāṉoṉṟit
       taṇṭuṭaṉ ōṭit talaippaṭṭa yōkikku
       maṇṭala mūṉṟiṉum okka vaḷarntapiṉ
       piṇṭamum ūḻi piriyā tirukkumē.

   - tirumantiram - 3-612, tiyāṉam
   Perfect Meditation Leads to Immortality
   Having abated not in the rules of vows,
   The Yogi that has to mediate learned,
   Coursing Kundalini through spinal column
   And passing Mandalas Three with felicity equal
   He in fleshy body forever lives.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   pēcum mantiram
   iṭaikalai, piṅkalai, cuḻumuṉaikaḷiṉ cuvācattāl uṇṭākum cattamē ajapā
   (japikkāmal eḻum oli) eṉappeṟum. 'sō' eṉum acapaiyaic civanāmam eṉac
   jepippar. pirāṇaṉai uṭkoḷḷumpōtu sō eṉa ēṉṟu, ham eṉa niṟuttit
   tiyāṉittalāl, itu sōham eṉap peṟum; itu hamsa mantiram eṉavum,
   civōham eṉavum, ūmaiyeḻuttu eṉavum peyar peṟum.
   intac cātaṉaiyil, cuḻumuṉaiyil ēṟum kuṇṭalik kaṉal. atu kiḷarnteḻum
   pōtu, takataka eṉṟu narampukaḷil tīkkaṉalum. nāvil mantiram cuḻalvatu
   pōṉṟa uṇarvu utikkum. kiḻakkil utittu mēṟku nōkki viraiyum paritipōl,
   uḷavaṟintu, cuṭarvaḻiyil cellum uyir. innilaiyil mēluraitta ajapā
   mantiram, veḷippaṭaiyāka uḷḷiruntu olikkum.
   41. kuṇṭali yataṉil kūṭiya acapai
   42. viṇṭeḻu mantiram veḷippaṭa uraittu
   patavurai:
   kuṇṭali ataṉil - kuṇṭaliṉi eṉum caktiyuṭaṉ,
   kūṭiya acapai - kūṭiyirukkum (uraikka muṭiyāta)
   amca manuvai,
   viṇṭu eḻu mantiram - vāy tiṟantu collum mantiramāka,
   veḷippaṭa uraittu - veḷippaṭaiyākak kūṟaṟku uriyatupōl kūṟi (eṉṟavāṟu).
   vāy tiṟantu uraikka iyalātatu, ajapā mantiram. itu kuṇṭaliṉiyiṉ
   uḷḷoḷiyāy aṭaṅkip ‘pēcāta mantiram’ eṉum peyaraip peṟṟatu.
   ataṉiliruntu pēcum mantiram piṟantatu. iraṇṭum taṉittaṉi ōreḻuttu
   mantiraṅkaḷ. ivaikaḷai veḷippaṭaiyāka eṉakkuk kaṇapati viḷaṅka
   upatēcittār eṉṟapaṭi,
   'acapai yaṟintuḷḷa aṉaleḻa nōkkil
       icaiyātu maṇṇil piṟappu'

   - eṉum auvai kuṟaḷum.
   'ōṅkāri eṉpā ḷavaḷoru peṇpiḷḷai
       nīṅkāta paccai niṟattai yuṭaiyavaḷ
       āṅkāri yākiyē aivaraip peṟṟiṭṭu
       rīṅkārat tuḷḷē yiṉitirun tāḷē!'

   - eṉkiṟār, mūlam uṇarnta tirumūlar. avar mantiramum iṅku maṉaṉam
   koḷḷaṟku uriyaṉa. iṉiya mūccai iḻuppar; niṟuttuvar; piṉ veḷiviṭuvar.
   akkālattil piṟakkum makattāṉa nuṇmai oliyē acapā mantiram eṉpatai,
   mīṭṭum niṉaivuṟuttukiṟōm. ūmai mantiram eṉṟum itaṟkup peyar uṇṭu.
   mēlum itu kuṟittu,
   'aintak karattiṉ arumporuḷ taṉṉai
       neñcak karuttiṉ nilaiyaṟi vittu'

   - eṉum 69'm aṭiyilum viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷatu; āṇṭum ataiyaṟital nalam.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   taṉpāl ulakun taṉakkaru kāvatum
       aṉpā leṉakkaru ḷāvatu māvaṉa
       eṉpārkaḷ ñāṉamum eytuñ civōkamum
       piṉpāliṉ nēyamum peṟṟiṭun tāṉē.

   - tirumantiram - 5-1469, ñāṉam
   Stages of Attainment Through Jnana
   Thus they say;
   By devotion the Jiva first sojourns Lord's world;
   Then comes to dwell in Lord's proximity;
   Further on receives Lords grace,
   And in the end attains Jnana
   In Sivohamic I and You union
   Jiva shall himself Siva become.
   irukkuñcēma iṭampirama mākum
       varukkañ carācara mākum ulakan
       tarukkiya ācāra mellān tarumē
       tirukkilā ñāṉattait tērntuṇarn tōrkkē.

   - tirumantiram - 5-1470, ñāṉam
   Unitive Attainment of Jnani
   Brahman shall be his impregnable abode,
   Universe, his kith and kin;
   Diverse paths the world presents
   All, all shall be his;
   For, verily he has realized
   The pure Jnana, free of doubt.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   citkaṉal
   'kaṉal iruvakai. avai parumaik kaṉal, nuṇmaik kaṉal eṉappeṟum.
   viṟakerikka, viḷakkēṟṟa, cāṇai piṭikkat tuḷḷi eḻuvatu tūlakkaṉal.
   uṉṉuḷ uḷḷatu nuṇmaikkaṉal. aṉaittaiyum ākkik kāppatu akkaṉal. ataṉai
   eḻuppi, mēru taṇṭattiṉ mēlēṟṟu! piramarantirattil muṭṭaccey! uyarnta
   pēriṉpam appōtē uṇṭākum. uṉṉil karantirukkum teyva caktikaḷ, appoḻutē
   kaṭṭaviḻntu kāṇa ilaṅkum; aruḷ oḷi tulaṅkum; aṟivāy ataṉai!' eṉṟu mēlum
   aṟivittāṉām attaṉ.
   43. mūlātārattiṉ mūṇṭeḻu kaṉalaik
   44. kālāl eḻuppum karuttaṟi vittē
   patavurai:
   mūla ātārattiṉ - aṭiccakkarameṉum mūlātārattiliruntu,
   mūṇṭu eḻu kaṉalai - paṟṟi eriyum (vintu eṉum) ñāṉak kaṉalai,
   kālāl eḻuppum - (pūraka rēcaka kumpakam eṉum) kāṟṟāl mēlēṟac ceyyum,
   karuttu aṟivittē - karuttai aṭiyēṟku uṇartti aruḷi (eṉṟavāṟu).
   iṭam valam cuḻumuṉai mūṉṟum, mūlātārattil pōy muṭikiṉṟaṉa. iṭai
   piṅkalaiyil pōkkuvaravu puriyum kuṇamāṉa kāṟṟaik kumpikka, kumpitta
   kāṟṟu, mūlakkaṉalai mēleḻuppum. annilaiyil, uṟaṅkiyirunta kuṇṭaliṉi,
   veppāṟṟātu viḻikkum. iṭai piṅkalai aṭaipaṭṭiruttaliṉ, cuḻumuṉai vaḻiyē
   mēlēṟum. ataṉuṭaṉ toṭarum āṉma uṇarvu, ātāra cakkaraṅkaḷaiyum,
   aṅkuḷa teyvaṅkaḷaiyum aṟiyum eṉpatu karuttu.
   mūlakkaṉalai uyirppāl mūḷa vaittapiṉ, aṟivil vantu taṅkum aruḷ;
   atu taṉṉaiyum aṟivikkum; uyti vaḻiyaiyum uṇarttum.
   'kālāl vaḻi taṭavum kālattē, kaṇmuḷaittāl
       pōlē, eṉataṟiviṟ pōntaṟivāy nillāyō'

   - eṉum tāyumāṉār pirārttaṉai, itaṉai mey mey eṉṟu
   meyppikkiṉṟatu allavā!
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   mēlai nilattiṉāḷ vētakap peṇpiḷḷai
       mūla nilattil eḻukiṉṟa mūrttiyai
       ēla eḻuppi ivaḷuṭaṉ cantikkap
       pālaṉum āvāṉ parānanti āṇaiyē.

   - tirumantiram - 3-590 - tāraṇai
   kālālē kaṉal ēṟṟuṅkaṭi cuḻi mēlē koṇṭa mutūṭṭuṅkaṭi
       mūlā tārat talaṅkēcara meṉṟu muḻaṅkik kummiyaṭiyuṅkaṭi!
       cārntu koḷḷaṭi kēcarattai mutaṟṟaṉṉai yaṟiyalān tāṉākak
       kūrntu mūlak kaṇapati pātattaik kumpiṭṭuk koḷḷaṭi ñāṉappeṇṇē!

   - maturai vālaiccāmi ñāṉakkummi - 66/67
   ātāra mūlat taṭiyil kaṇapatiyaip
       pātāra vintam paṇintu niṟpatu ekkālam!
       maṇvaḷainta naṟkīṟṟil vaḷaintirunta vētāvaik
       kaṇvaḷarttu pārttuḷḷē kaṇṭiruppatu ekkālam!
       appup piṟai naṭuvē amarntirunta viṭṭuṇuvai
       uppuk kuṭukkaiyuḷḷē uṇarntaṟivatu ekkālam!
       mūṉṟu vaḷaiyam iṭṭu muḷaitteḻunta kōṇattil
       tōṉṟum uruttiraṉait toḻutu niṟpatu ekkālam!
       vāyu aṟu kōṇam atil vāḻum makēccuraṉait
       tōyum vakai kēṭkat toṭaṅkuvatum ekkālam!
       vaṭṭa vaḻikkuḷḷē maruvum catācivattaic
       kiṭṭa vaḻitēṭak kirupai ceyvatu ekkālam!

   - pattirakiriyār meyññāṉap pulampal - 66-70
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   amuta nilai
   mutaṉmaic cātaṉai mutira, piṟaṅkiya ānantam perukum; atil aḻuttum
   amayam, uḷḷattil tuḷḷum oḷi uṇarvu. catāciva nilaiyai ōṅkāra pāttiram
   eṉṟu kūṟuvar. tikaḻum mati maṇṭalattil, cuḻaṉṟu kaṉalum tīccuvālai.
   ataṉāl urukum amutam, anta ōṅkāra pāttirattil ūṟṟeṭukkum.
   'mūla neruppai yiṭṭu muṭṭinilā maṇṭapattil
       pālai yiṟakki yuṇṭu pacioḻivatu ekkālam!'

   - eṉṟu pattirakiriyār pāṭuvatu pōl, anta amutam paruki niṟaivuṟukiṟatu
   āṉmā. cantiraṉukku uriyatu patiṉāṟu kalai. atupōl, puruvanaṭuvum
   patiṉāṟu piriviṉatu; aṅku ūṟum amutattaip parukap paruka, uyarnta
   pēraṟivu utikkiṉṟatu. itaṉuṭaṉ, kumuṟa vaitta āṉmāviṉ paruvuṭal
   nuṇṇuṭal kuṟaiyum tīrum. ivvamayam, 'aruḷamutam puriyāyēl, varuntuvaṉ
   ittamiyēṉ maṟṟu eṉṉē nāṉ āmāṟē' eṉum tiruvācakamum niṉaiviṟ pōntu, iṉṟu
   varumō allatu maṟṟu eṉṟu varumō
   ippēṟu eṉṟu nammai eṇṇa vaikkiṉṟatu allavā!
   tirikaraṇa vaḻipāṭṭil teyva amutam tiraḷātēl, iṟappum piṟappum
   iṭarppāṭum eṉṟum iruppatu iyalpu. ataṉāl, toṭarntu tiyāṉippar aṟavōr.
   accārpiṟku uriyatu tiyāṉa camāti.
   intac cutta camāti, cittam urukkum. avvamayam, uttamamāṉa tāṉ,
   ucciyiliruntu ottu ūṟum. atai yōkikaḷ amutapāṉam eṉpar. itaip
   parukuvārtam kuṇṭalikkaṉal, piramarantiram varai eṭṭum; mūla ātārattil
   kālūṉṟi vittaka vinōtamāka viḷaiyāṭum eṉkiṉṟār vimala kaṇapati.
   45. amuta nilaiyum ātittaṉ iyakkamum
   46. kumuta cakāyaṉ kuṇattaiyum kūṟi
   patavurai:
   amuta nilaiyum - citkaṉal, cuḻumuṉai vaḻiyēṟic cirattil uḷḷa
   piramarantira
   mati maṇṭalam cārntu ūṟum amutattai uṇṭākkum nilaiyaiyum,
   ātittaṉ iyakkamum - (kāyattiṟ cantiraṉuṭaṉum, akṉiyuṭaṉum kalantu,
   cutta māyaiyaic cārntu) cūriyaṉākiya piṅkalai ūrtalaiyum,
   kumuta cakāyaṉ kuṇattaiyum kūṟi - āmpal malarttōḻaṉ ākiya cantiraṉ
   (cūriyaṉil cārntuḷḷa) paṇpaiyum aṟivuṟutti (eṉṟavāṟu).
   mūṉṟu maṇṭalam uṭalil mutirntuḷatu. mūlātārattiliruntu iruviral
   aḷaviṟkumēl nāṉku kōṇam; naṭuvē oru mukkōṇam; aṅkē nāṉku itaḻkkamalam;
   kaṉalkiṟatu ataṉil kaṉal maṇṭalam.
   untikku nālu viral aḷaviṟku mēl. itaya kamalattil āṟukōṇamāṉa eṭṭitaḻk
   kamalam irukkiṉṟatu. itu makattāṉa ñāyiṟu maṇṭalam.
   talai naṭuvil, oḷi vaḻaṅkum amuta kalaiyuṭaṉ tiṅkaḷ maṇṭalam
   tikaḻkiṟatu. atu kīḻ nōkkip poḻiyum amutattai, nāpiyiṉ mēluḷḷa viḻumiya
   pariti maṇṭalam viḻuṅki viṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉāltāṉ, uṭalil narai tirai
   mūppukaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.
   talainaṭuvāṉa piramarantirattil irukkum āyiram itaḻt tāmaraiyai, māṇpār
   civacūriyaṉāl malarac ceytu, ak kamalap pūntātuvil kaṭiṉap paṭṭirukkum
   tiṅkaḷ maṇṭalattai mūla neruppiṉāl iḷaka vaittu, aṅkiruntu payaṉ tara
   urukip pāyum uyarnta amutattai, tam ākkaiyil uḷḷa nāṭikaḷ aṉaittiṉum
   paravac ceyvatu yōkanaṭai.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   mūlā tāramo ṭēṟṟi yaṅkiyai
       āṟā tāramo ṭōṭṭi yantira
       mūlā vāyuvai yēṟṟu naṉcuḻi muṉaiyūṭē
       mūtā tārama ruppi lantara
       nātā kītama tārtti ṭumpara
       mūṭē pāloḷi āttu mantaṉai vilakāmal
       mālā ṭūṉoṭu cērtti tampeṟa
       nāṉā vētama cātti rañcolum
       vāḻñā ṉāpuri yēṟṟi mantira tavicūṭē
       mātā nātaṉum vīṟṟi runtiṭum
       vīṭē mūṇoḷi kāṭṭi cantira
       vākār tēṉamu tūṭṭi yeṉṟaṉai yuṭaṉāḷvāy!

   - aruṇakiriyār - vaittīcuraṉ kōyil tiruppukaḻ
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   cantira maṇṭala cahasrārattiṉ amutanilai aṉupavam cittittatum, iṭai
   piṅkalai iyakkamum māṟum. pacupōtam pāḻākum; civapōtam cērum. mēlāṉa
   iccātaṉaiyai mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷ, itu kaivalyam āka iṭaiyaṟātu muyalvar.
   'niyama laṭcaṇamum iyama laṭcaṇamum
       āca ṉātivita pētamum
       neṭitu ṇarttiya patma pīṭamicai
       niṉṟi laṅkum acapā nalattu
       iyala ṟintuvaḷar mūla kuṇṭaliyai
       iṉiti ṟaiñci avaḷ aruḷiṉāl
       ellai yaṟṟuvaḷar cōti mūlaaṉal
       eṅkaḷ mōṉamaṉu muṟaiyilē
       vayami kuntuvarum amarta maṇṭala
       matikkuḷē matiyai vaittu nāṉ
       vāyma ṭuttu amarta vāri yaipparuki
       maṉṉum āramirta vaṭivamāyc
       ceyami kuntuvaru citta yōkanilai
       peṟṟu ñāṉa neṟi yaṭaivēṉō
       teriva taṟkariya pirama mēamala
       ciṟka kōtaya vilācamē!'

   - eṉṟu vimalaṉai aḻutu kataṟi vēṇṭuvar. ataṉuṭaṉ aṉupavam paḻutta
   parama ñāṉikaḷāṉa cittarkaṇattaiyum kumuṟiyaḻutu kūppiṭṭu, emateṇṇam
   niṟaivēṟa varavēṇṭum; vantu vaḻi aruḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu vēṇṭuvatum uṇṭu.
   eṇṇariya piṟavitaṉil māṉiṭap piṟavitāṉ
       yātiṉum aritaritukāṇ
       ippiṟavi tappiṉāl eppiṟavi vāykkumō
       ētu varumō aṟikilēṉ;
       kaṇṇaka nilattunāṉ uḷḷa poḻutē aruḷ
       kakaṉa vaṭṭattuḷ niṉṟu
       kālūṉṟi niṉṟu poḻi āṉanta mukiloṭu
       kalantumati avaca muṟavē
       paṇṇuvatu naṉmai; in nilaipatiyu maṭṭumē
       patiyā yirunta tēkap
       pavuri kulaiyāmalē kauri, kuṇṭali, āyi,
       paṇṇavitaṉ aruḷiṉālē
       viṇṇilavu matiyamutam oḻiyātu poḻiyavē
       vēṇṭuvēṉ umataṭimai nāṉ;
       vētānta cittānta camaracanaṉ ṉilaipeṟṟa
       vittakac cittar kaṇamē'

   - eṉum tāyumāṉāriṉ ariya ip pirārttaṉaikaḷ, arumaiyāṉa nuṭpa vaḻikaḷai
   namakku aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa allavā! avvaḷavutāṉā! iṉṉum pāruṅkaḷ!
   cantira cūriyar eṉum iṭai piṅkalaikaḷ oṉṟu cērumpōtu, nātanilai naṇṇum.
   cantiraṉ eṉum vintukalai, puruva naṭuvāṉa ākñā cakkara ñāṉākkiṉi
   kalaiyil aṭaṅkum.
   cantira oḷi tōṉṟāta amāvācai, kēvala nilaiyaiyum kuṟikkum. matiyait
   taṉṉiṭam aṭakkiya paritiyiṉ cuttamāyā oḷiyil iruppavarkku, iravum
   pakalum eṉṟum illai.
   cantira oḷiyil kumutam malartaliṉ, matiyavaṉaik kumuta cakāyaṉ eṉṟu
   kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār auvaiyār.
   'vintuvum nātamum mēruvum ōṅkiṭil
       cantiyil āṉa camātiyil kūṭiṭum
       antam ilāta aṟiviṉ arumporuḷ
       cuntarac cōtiyum tōṉṟiṭun tāṉē'
       'cantiraṉ cūriyaṉ tāṉvaril pūcaṉai
       muntiya pānuvil intuvan(tu) ēymuṟai
       anta iraṇṭum upaya nilattil
       cintai teḷintār civam āyiṉārē'

   - eṉṟu tirumūlarum,
   'nilāmaṇ ṭalattil niṟainta amutuṇṇil
       ulāvalām antarattiṉ mēl.'
       'aṇṇākkut taṉṉai yaṭaittaṅ kamirtuṇṇil
       viṇṇōrkku vēntaṉu mām.'
       'īreṇ kalaiyiṉ niṟainta amirtuṇṇil
       pōṅkālam illai purintu.'
       'āṉa kalacat tamirtai yaṟintuṇṇil
       pōṉakam vēṇṭāmal pōm.'
       'ūṟum amirtai uṇṭiyuṟap pārkkil
       kūṟum piṟappaṟakka lām.'
       'mēlai yamirtai vilakkāmal tāṉuṇṇil
       kālaṉai vañcikka lām.'

   - eṉa itu kuṟittu auvaiyār aruḷiyuḷḷa kuṟaḷpākkaḷum eṉṟum eṇṇi
   iṉpuṟutaṟku uriyaṉa.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   uṇkalām piramatti laṭaṅkum pōtē
       uṟutiyuḷḷa aṇṭatti lurukip pāyun
       tiṅkalān tōṇumaṭā amirtac cīṉi
       tittippup pāluṭaṉē tiṭamāy maintā!
       taṅkalān tēkamatu aḻiyā maṟṟāṉ
       caṭṭaiyumē kaḻaṉṟumikat taṅkam pōlē
       poṅkalām meyññāṉat tīpat tālē
       pūrittup pārttiṭavē puvaṉa moṉṟē!

   - cittar kākapucuṇṭar
   teḷivuṟum aṟiviṉai nām koṇṭu
       cērttaṉam niṉakkatu cōmaracam,
       oḷiyuṟum uyircceṭiyil - itai
       ōṅkiṭum mativali taṉiṟpiḻintōm.
       kaḷiyuṟak kuṭittiṭuvāy - niṉṟaṉ
       kaḷinaṭaṅ kāṇpataṟ kuḷaṅkaṉintōm;
       kuḷircuvaip pāṭṭicaittē - curar
       kuḷattiṉiṟ cērntiṭal virumpukiṉṟōm.

   - mahākavi pārati
   St.TiruMular sings on this ‘Somarasa’ in Tantra –3 - on Kechari Yaga
   Aynthurai seyyil amuthaninR uRIdum
   vAynthurai seyyum varukinRa kAlaththu
   nInthurai seyyil nilA mandalam athAy –p-
   paynthurai seythu pAlikkumARE.
   If skillfully rubbed by the tongue's tip
   The mystic nectar will begin to ooze;
   When it comes, with care manage it
   That you may swim in Moon's Region mystic;
   And that which flowed and roared
   May preserved be.
   This is the ultimate yogic technique or Mudra called Kechari Mudra.
   Once the Kundalini is roused and all the adharas are pervaded, a
   perfect Yogi reaches stage of drinking in the divine madhu / nectar
   that flows from the astral sphere, known in Yogic language as nilA
   mandalam – piRai or Soma – within. At this stage the Yogi reaches the
   Samadhi stage, locking the breath within the body. And he no longer
   needs any external food to live on. Literally Kechari means to traverse
   in the sky.
   Quote:
   Hatha Yoga Pradipika (iii, 41 – 53):
   The siddhas have named this mudra, Kechari, from the fact that the mind
   and the tongue reach the akasa (ether) by its practice.
   If the Yogi drinks Somarasa - vital fluid - by sitting with the tongue
   turned backwards and mind concentrated, there is no doubt he conquers
   death within 15 days. If the Yogi, whose body is full of Somarasa, were
   bitten by KingCobra too, its poison can not permeate his body. As fire
   is inseparably connected with the wood, the soul does not leave the
   body full of nectar exuding from the soma.
   It is mentioned in Vedas that those who eat the flesh of the cow and
   drink the immortal somabana daily are regarded as men of noble family
   or Aryans.
   The word goar (cow) means tongue; eating is thwarting it in the gullet.
   Immortal liquor is the nectar exuding from the moon. It is produced by
   the fire, which is generated by thrusting tongue. If the tongue can
   touch with its end the hole from which falls the rasa which is saltish,
   bitter, sour, milky and similar * to a sort of mixture of ghee and
   honey, one can drive away all diseases. There is only one seed
   germinating the whole universe from it called Aum; and there is only
   one mudra called Kechari.
   Unquote
   The pathway to this ultimate salvation is hidden in St.Avvaiyar’s
   Vinayagar Agaval.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   patiṉāṟu nilaiyum uṟuppum
   cutta attuvā, acutta attuvā, cutta acuttam iraṇṭum āṉa (misra māyai)
   attuvā eṉa, mūṉṟu nilaikaḷ eṉṟum ūṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. tūya peruvaḻi,
   tūymaiyaṟṟa peruvaḻi, iraṇṭum kalanta peruvaḻi eṉṉum poruḷaṉa ivai.
   ivaikaḷuṭaṉ, pala vēṟu kiḷai vaḻikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa.
   taṉakkeṉa amainta āṟṟalāl toḻilpaṭum iyantirampōl tūya attuvāvil
   uḷḷa civa tattuvamum, cutta vityā tattuvamum, ñāṉacaktiyāl viṟuviṟu
   eṉṟu
   viraintu cuḻalkiṉṟaṉa.
   cakti tattuvamum cātākkiyat tattuvamum, kiriyā caktiyālum, īsvara
   tattuvam,
   iṉitāka eḻum iccā caktiyālum iṉitu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
   civatattuvam nāta vaṭivāki, acutta attuvāvil naṇṇiya māyaiyai
   naṭattukiṉṟatu.
   cutta vityā tattuvam, vittaiyai āṉmāviṟku virivāka viḷaṅka vaikkum.
   cakti tattuvam vintu vaṭivamāy, kālam, niyati, kalai eṉṉum mūṉṟu
   tattuvaṅkaḷaiyum muṉṉēṟac ceyyum.
   cātākyat tattuvam puruṭa tattuvattai naṭattum.
   īsvara tattuvam arāka tattuvattai muṉṉēṟṟum.
   vittai, putti tattuvattai viḷakkum.
   arākam, maṉat tattuvattaic celuttum.
   maṉam maṉṉaṉāki iruntu aṟikaruvi toḻilkaruvikaḷai nalamuṟat taṉ pōkkil
   naṭattum.
   iṅṅaṉam karuvikaḷai iyakki, āṉmā aimpula viṣayaṅkaḷaic civacakti
   aṉupavikkac ceyyum.
   ivaikaḷē tattuva iyakkam eṉappeṟum. inta iyakkam nilaiyilātatu eṉalai
   iṉitu uṇartti, nilaiyāṉa civayōkattil aḻuttaṟ poruṭṭē uṭal cakkaram
   uruḷukiṉṟatu. uruḷum camayam, uṭal uṟuppukaḷ ovvoṉṟum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa.
   itaip pāṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār nam pāṭṭiyār.
   47. iṭaic cakkarattiṉ īreṭṭu nilaiyum
   48. uṭal cakkarattiṉ uṟuppaiyum kāṭṭi
   patavurai:
   iṭaic cakkarattiṉ - (āṟu ātāraṅkaḷiṉ) naṭuvāka iruntu iyaṅkukiṉṟa
   (kaṇṭa)
   cakkarattiṉ,
   īreṭṭu nilaiyum - patiṉāṟu kalai (itaḻkaḷuṭaṉ irukkum) nilaikaḷaiyum,
   uṭal cakkarattiṉ - uṭalākiya iyantirattil uḷḷa,
   uṟuppaiyum kāṭṭi - (oṉṟai oṉṟu iyakkum) aṅkaṅkaḷaiyum kāṇumāṟu aṟivittu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   cahasrāram - 1
   ātāram - 6
   lalāṭapintu - 1
   artta cantiraṉ - 1
   rōkiṉi - 1
   nātam - 1
   nātāntam - 1
   cakti - 1
   viyāpikā - 1
   camaṉā - 1
   uṉmaṉā - 1
   ivai iṭaiccakkaram eṉappeṟum. ippatiṉāṟum kaṭantavarē mēlōr.
   ātārac cakkaraṅkaḷ āṟum, cūriya cantira kalaiyiraṇṭum cērntu, uṭal
   cakkarattiṉ
   uṟuppukaḷ eṉappeṟum. uṟuppukaḷ palavaṟṟāl, toḻil ceyyum cakkarampōl
   irukkiṉṟatu uṭal. ataṉāl ataṉai iyantiramāka uruvakittār.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   kālamām vaṉattilaṇṭak kōlamā marattiṉ mītu
       kāḷicakti yeṉṟapeyar koṇṭu - rīṅ
       kāramiṭ ṭulavumoru vaṇṭu - taḻal
       kālum viḻi nīlavaṇṇa mūlaattu vākkaḷeṉum
       kālkaḷā ṟuṭaiya teṉak kaṇṭu - maṟai
       kāṇumuṉi vōruraittār paṇṭu.

   - pārati - mahākāḷiyiṉ pukaḻ
   kālameṉum peruvaṉattil, oli oḷikaḷālēyē
   aṇṭaṅkaḷum piṇṭaṅkaḷum ākki oṭukkap paṭuvaṉa.
   nātam eṉṉum olivakaiyil eḻuttu, patam, maṟaikaḷ eṉṟum,
   vintu eṉum oḷivakaiyil kalai, tattuvam, puvaṉam eṉṟum
   aṉaittumē aṟuvaḻikaḷāl, atvākkaḷāl āvaṉa.
   avaṟṟai iyakkiyirukkum aṉṉaiyai vaṇaṅkuvōm!
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   vaṭivat tiyāṉam
   cakuṇam - parumai; itu āṟu aḷaviṉatu. itai vikrakam eṉpar.
   nirkkuṇam - nuṇmai; itu nāṉku aḷaviṉatu. itai yantirattil
   amaittup peṟuvatu marapu. ip pattu nilaikaḷum, tiyāṉattiṉ mūlam
   cittikkum eṉṟum aṟivittatu āṉaimukat teyvam.
   49. caṇmuka tūlamum caturmuka cūkkamum
   50. eṇmukam āka iṉiteṉakku aruḷi
   patavurai:
   caṇmuka tūlamum - āṟumukamākiya parumaip poruḷāṉa iyalpaiyum,
   caturmuka cūkkamum - nāṉkumuka nuṇporuḷāṉa iyalpaiyum,
   eṇmukamāka - eṇṇum tiyāṉa iṭamāka,
   iṉitu eṉakku aruḷi - iṉimaiyāka aṭiyēṉukku upatēcittu (eṉṟavāṟu).
   uruva vaḻipāṭu uyarnta naṭai. nampaṉukku mukam nāṉku. āṟumukaṅkaḷum
   amaiyum. kayamukaṉaic cāykka oru mukattuṭaṉ kāṭciyāyiṉar kaṇapati.
   ēkamūrtti; nāl ticaikkum nātaṉ; āṟu ticaikkum ammāṉ; eṇ ticaikkum
   uriyaṉ emmāṉ;
   pattu ticaikkum paramaṉ.
   'pattut tikkum paṇi nutaṟkaṇ tiruvāḷaṉ' eṉṟu kacinta maṉattuṭaṉ
   pāṭukiṟār kapilar.
   iṅṅaṉam tam paripākattiṟkut takkapaṭi uru amaittu, mukam amaittu,
   ārātippar nallaṭiyārkaḷ. aṉpāl eṇṇiya mukattoṭu, avarmuṉ eḻuntaruḷvar
   nam hērampar. ivvaricaiyuḷ, caṇmuka tūlamum caturmuka cūkkamum,
   yōkikaṭku eṇmukamāka iruppaṉa; avaikaḷiṉ nuṭpamē nam pāṭṭikku aṟivikkap
   peṟṟaṉa. inta upatēcaṅkaḷai nāmum iṅku eṇṇal nalam.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   āciriyar intap pakutiyai mikac curukkamāyc collic celkiṟār. ivviṭam
   mēlum cila viḷakkaṅkaḷaic colla viḻaikiṟēṉ. potuvāy, caṇmukam, caṭkōṇam
   eṉṟālē civacakti aikkiyattaic cuṭṭum eḷimaiyāṉa kōlam. yantiraṅkaḷ
   palavakai eṉṟālum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa vaṭivam, mēlum kīḻumāy iru mukkōṇaṅkaḷ
   piṇaintu uruvākkum ikkōlam. itaip pūcaiyil maṭṭumiṉṟi veḷiyē
   mākkōlattil varaintum vaṇaṅkuvatu paṇṭai vaḻakku. ippaṭit tattam maṉam
   oṉṟiya vaṭivattai vaṇaṅkip piṉ, vaṇaṅkuvōṉum, vaṇakkamum, vaṇaṅkap
   paṭuvatum āṉa mūṉṟu nilaikaḷum oṉṟāki uṭkuvivatu ataṉ aṭuttapaṭi.
   viṭṭakuṟai, toṭṭakuṟaiyāy mīṇṭumoru piṟaviyil vantuviḻum ñāṉiyarkku
   itu tēvaiyillai. kutampaic cittariṉ ariya pāṭaloṉṟaik kāṇpōm:
   mukkōṇam taṉṉil muḷaitta meyññāṉikkuc
   caṭkōṇam ētukkaṭi kutampāy!
   caṭkōṇam ētukkaṭi!
   uṇmuka taricaṉattil, nālitaḻ koṇṭa caturattukkuḷ amainta, kīḻ nōkkiya
   mukkōṇamāṉa kuṇṭalik kaṉalai mēlnōkki eḻuppum meyññāṉikku, puṟavaṭivat
   tūṇṭutal ētukkaṭi eṉṟu iṅkē pāṭukiṟār kutampaiyār.
   pārata ñāṉamarapil iṟaivaṉai akattuḷ aṟivatē iṟutippaṭi.
   iṟaivaṉ tūṇilum iruppavaṉ, turumpilum iruppavaṉ eṉṟu potuvāyc colvatu
   vaḻakku. appaṭi avaṉai uṇarntavarkē uḷaṉ. aṉṟēl alaṉ eṉṟu tirumūlar
   pāṭum ariya pāṭaloṉṟaik kāṇpōm:
   māṭattu ḷāṉalaṉ maṇṭapat tāṉalaṉ
       kūṭattu ḷāṉalaṉ kōyiluḷ ḷāṉalaṉ
       vēṭattu ḷāṉalaṉ vēṭkaiviṭ ṭārneñcil
       mūṭattu ḷēniṉṟu muttitan tāṉē!

   (tirumantiram - 2614)
   mūṭam eṉṟāl meyyuṭal.
   ivviṭam tirumūlar uṇarttuvatu 'tammuḷ iṟai uṇarāmal vēṟeṅku tēṭiṉum
   kāṇpataritu' eṉpatē. tammuḷ uṇarvōrkkē avaṉ eṅkumuḷaṉ; eṉṟumuḷaṉ.
   taṉṉuḷ ataiyuṇarnta pirakalātaṉ viḷikka uṭaṉ paramporuḷ tūṇiliruntum
   vaṭiveṭuttu vantatu avvaṇṇamē.
   aṅṅaṉam aṉṟi irumaiyil avaṉait taṉittut tēṭuvārkku akappaṭuvōṉ allaṉ.
   itai valiyuṟutti civañāṉapōtattiṉ iraṇṭām cūttirattiṉ nāṉkām
   atikaraṇattil viḷakka vanta meykaṇṭatēvariṉ pāṭalitu:
   eṅkumuḷa ṉeṉṟaḷavai oṉṟaṉ ṟiraṇṭeṉṉil
   eṅku muḷaṉaṉ ṟevaṟṟevaṉum - aṅkaṇ
   avaiyavaṉ aṉṟillai poṉṉoḷipōl īcaṉ
   avaiyuṭaimai āḷām nām aṅku.
   eṅkum uḷaṉ eṉṟa aḷavai oṉṟaṉṟu iraṇṭu eṉil eṅkum uḷaṉ aṉṟu!
   katiravaṉaiyum katiroḷiyum pōl avaṉ pirikkaviyalātavaṉ.
   aruntuyark kurampaiyiṉ āṉmā nāṭi eḻunta civañāṉapōtattiṉ cāramāy
   attēṭaliṉ muṭivil meykaṇṭatēvar cuṭṭuvatum a·tē.
   'avaṉē tāṉē ākiya anneṟi
   ēkaṉāki iṟaipaṇi niṟka
   malamāyai taṉṉōṭu valviṉai iṉṟē'
   eṉṟa pattām cūttirattiṉ mutal atikaraṇa viḷakkam:
   'īṇṭup paramēcuvaraṉ ivvāṉmāvāy niṉṟa muṟaimaiyāṉ
   avaṉiṭattu ēkaṉāki niṟka eṉṟatu'. piṉṉar iṟaipaṇi niṟṟalum
   cāttiyamē.
   civavākkiyar aruḷiya iraṇṭu pāṭalkaḷaip pārppōm. nāttikat toṉiyil
   iruppatāy palar tavaṟāyc cuṭṭum pāṭalkaḷ ivai:
   naṭṭa kallait teyvam eṉṟu nālu puṭpam cāttiyē
       cuṟṟi vantu muṇumuṇeṉṟu collu mantram ētaṭā!
       naṭṭa kallum pēcumō nātaṉ uḷḷirukkaiyil!
       cuṭṭa caṭṭi caṭṭuvam kaṟiccuvai aṟiyumō?
       kōyil āvatu ētaṭā kuḷaṅkaḷ āvatu ētaṭā?
       kōyilum kuḷaṅkaḷum kumpiṭum kulāmarē
       kōyilum maṉattuḷē kuḷaṅkaḷum maṉattuḷē
       āvatum aḻivatum illai illai illaiyē!

   nātaṉ uḷḷiruppatai uṇarntāl naṭṭakallum teyvamākum; kōyil maṟṟum
   kuḷattiṉ cūkkumam uṇarntāl avaṟṟiṉ cānnityam ciṟakkat tulaṅkum eṉpatē
   avar cuṭṭuvatu.
   mēṟkaṇṭa viḷakkam, paktiyoṉṟait tavira vēṟoṉṟum aṟiyāta kaṇṇappa
   nāyaṉārukkup poruntumā?
   cālap poruntum.
   'yāṉ eṉatu' eṉṟa mayakkaṟutta, tām taḻuvi niṟpatu veṟum kallaṉṟu,
   tammait
   taṭuttāṭ koṇṭa pēruyirē eṉṟa piṇaippil tam kaṇṇaip piṭuṅki appi
   *koḷkaiyiṉ umpar mēlār* eṉṟāki niṉṟa, tiṇṇaṉ eṉṟa anta makāyōkikku
   'vēṭkai viṭṭār neñcil mūṭattuḷē niṉṟu mutti tantāṉē!' eṉṟa pērurai
   cālap poruntum.
   ataṉāṟṟāṉ, campuvāṉa akkallum kacintu 'nillu kaṇṇappa!' eṉṟu
   taṭuttaṇaittu, anta aṉpuṭait tōṉṟalai, 'eṉ valattil māṟilāy! niṟka!'
   eṉṟaruḷ purintāṇṭu koṇṭatu.
   * koḷkaiyiṉ umpar mēlār* eṉṟu cēkkiḻār perumāṉtam tiruvākkāl
   eḻuntatu, kaṇṇappar 'yāṉ eṉatu' eṉṉum cerukkaṟuttu vāṉōrkkum uyarnta
   ulakam pukuntamaiyāl.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   eṭṭu nilai
   'taṉmāttiraik kottu itu. aimpūtak kottu itu. aṟikaruvik kottu itu.
   toḻiṟkaruvik kottu itu. antakkaraṇak kottu itu. mukkuṇak kottu itu.
   mūlap pakuti itu. kalaikaḷiṉ kottu itu. inta maṭṭilāta eṭṭuk kottukaḷum
   uṉṉil oṭṭiyiruttalai ūṉṟik kāṇ! itō pār! ātāram āṟu, nirātāram oṉṟu,
   mītāṉam oṉṟu āka eṭṭu nilai ivai. payaṉ tarum ivaikaḷaiyum pār!' eṉṟu,
   ittokutikaḷiṉ nilaiyaip pirittup pirittuk kāṇpittāy perumāṉē!
   ati cūkkumam āṉa uṭaliṉ nuṭpattai, nīr kāṇpitta ataṉ piṟakutāṉ aṟiya
   nērntēṉ eṉṟu auvaiyār aruḷvataiyum kēḷuṅkaḷ!
   51. puri aṭṭa kāyam pulappaṭa eṉakkut
   52. teri eṭṭu nilaiyum tericaṉap paṭutti
   patavurai:
   puri aṭṭakāyam - atinuṭpa eṭṭuk kottāṉa uṭaliṉ taṉmaikaḷ,
   eṉakkup pulappaṭa - aṭiyēṟkup pulaṉākumpaṭiyum,
   teri eṭṭunilaiyum - viḷaṅkum (ātāra nirātāra mītāṉamāṉa)
   eṭṭunilaikaḷum,
   tericaṉappaṭutti - kāṭciyākumpaṭic ceytu (eṉṟavāṟu).
   ivaikaḷaik kuṟittut tam tirumantirattil,
   'attaṉ amaitta uṭaliru kūṟiṉil
   cuttama tākiya cūkkumam colluṅkāl
   catta parica rūpa racakantam
   puttimāṉ āṅkāram puriyaṭṭa kāyamē'
   - eṉkiṟār tirumūlar.
   itaṉpaṭi, taṉaḷavu eṉum taṉmāttiraiyākiya aintum, putti, maṉam,
   āṅkāramākiya mūṉṟum cērntu puriyaṭṭa kāyam eṉap peṟukiṉṟatu.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   uraiyāciriyar ippakutiyil, puriyaṭṭa kāyattiṟkup palavitak kottukkaḷaic
   cuṭṭiṉālum, potuvāy, tirumantirattil maṭṭumiṉṟi, mēlum pala cittar
   pāṭalkaḷilum, meykaṇṭa cāttira nūlkaḷilum, iṟutiyāyc cuṭṭum
   taṉmāttiraikaḷ aintuṭaṉ cittam tavirtta maṉam, putti, akaṅkāram āṉa
   antakkaraṇat tokuppē cūkkuma uṭal, kaṉavuṭal eṉṟu pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
   iṉṉoru pāṭalaip pārppōm:
   eṭṭiṉil aintākum intiri yaṅkaḷum
       kaṭṭiya mūṉṟu karaṇamu māyviṭum
       oṭṭiya pāca uṇarveṉṉum kāyappai
       kaṭṭi aviḻttiṭuṅ kaṇṇutal kāṇumē.
       - tirumantiram 2-473, karu uṟpatti

   Of the eight organs of Body Subtle
   Are senses protean five
   And cognitive instruments three -
   Mind, will and cognition;
   Know the dear Lord
   Who fastened this body bag,
   With Desire's sticky glue
   Will in time unfasten it too.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   cālōkam
   mala iruḷ vilakum kālam varum. uyarnta annilaiyil uyir oḷiviḷakkam
   peṟum. avvamayam, cirattiṟku mēl paṉṉiraṇṭu aṅkulattil eḻunta pēroḷi
   taricaṉam piṟakkum. ic corupanilai cittitta cīvaṉ, ippēroḷiyait
   taricittup peṟuvatu cālōka patam. ñāṉattiṟ cariyaiyai mēṟkoṇṭār
   peṟukiṉṟa pēṟu itu.
   'nīṭum ciraciṭaip paṉṉiraṇ(ṭu) aṅkulam
       ōṭum uyireḻuttu ōṅki utittiṭa
       nāṭumiṉ nātānta namperumāṉ ukantu
       āṭum iṭamtiru ampalan tāṉē'

   - eṉpatu tirumantiram.
   53. karuttiṉil kapāla vāyil kāṭṭi
   54. irutti mutti iṉiteṉakku aruḷi
   patavurai:
   karuttiṉil - eṉatu ūṉṟi uṇar uḷḷattil,
   kapāla vāyil kāṭṭi - talaiyōṭṭiṉ tuḷaivaḻi mēluḷḷa tuvātacāntap
   peruveḷiyai viḷaṅkumpaṭi kāṇpittu,
   irutti - eṉṉai avveḷiyil cummā irukka vaittu,
   eṉakku iṉitu mutti aruḷi - aṭiyēṉukkuc cālōka cukattai
   iṉitāka aruḷi (eṉṟavāṟu).
   cummā iruttalai uṇarttum curuti pala.
   cummā eṉpatil, u-m-a eṉṉum mūṉṟu eḻuttukkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avai
   ōṅkārattiṉ uṭporuḷ; cakaram makarattōṭu cērntu, hamsa mantiram eṉum
   acapaiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. āṭum tiruvaṭiyai nāṭi niṉṟāraic cummā irukkum
   cukam tēṭi varum. cintaiyai aṭakkic cummā iruttal aṭṭa cittikaḷiṉum
   ariya oru citti. uḷḷattil teyvapporuḷ uḷatu. atai, ācai eṉum āṇavat
   tirai mūṭiyuḷatu. cummā irukkum nilai nīṭikka nīṭikka, vīṇāṉa attirai
   vilakum. annilaiyil teyva meypporuḷ teḷivāka viḷaṅkum.
   'ōrāmalē orukāl uṉṉāmal uḷḷoḷiyaip
       pārāmal uḷḷapaṭi pārttiruntāl - vārātō
       pattut ticaiyum paranteḻuntā ṉantaveḷḷam
       tattik karaipuraṇṭu tāṉ'

   - eṉṟapaṭi itaya citcapaiyil iṉitu tuḷḷūm teyva iṉpam. cummā irunta
   āṉmā, koḻikkum avviṉpattaik koḷḷum.
   'ētukkuc cummā irumaṉamē eṉṟu(u)ṉakkup
       pōtitta uṇmaiyeṅkē pōkaviṭṭāy - vātukku
       vantetirtta mallaraippōl vātāṭiṉāyē! uṉ
       puntiyeṉṉa pōtameṉṉa pō!'

   - eṉṟu maṉattiṟku upatēcittu, vāḻum vaḻiyil atai vaḻippaṭuttuvar mēlōr.
   'emmāl aṟivataṟa emperumāṉ yātumiṉṟic
       cummā irukkaoru cūttirantā ṉillaiyō!'
       'collum poruḷumaṟṟuc cummā iruppataṟku
       allum pakalum eṉak(ku) ācai parāparamē!'

   - eṉṟellām mutalvaṉiṭam appēṟu vāykka muṟaiyiṭuvar. ataṉ payaṉāka cummā
   irukkum cukam peṟuvar. emmatamum cammatikkum iṭam itutāṉ.
   'cummā iru collaṟa eṉṟalumē
       ammā poruḷoṉṟum aṟintilaṉē'

   - eṉpatu kantar anupūti.
   'mummāyaikku appālām muppāḻum kaṇṭu nī
       cummā iruveṉṟu coṉṉāṉ'
       - eṉkiṟatu oḻiviloṭukkam.

   'cummā irukkavaitta cūttirattai nāṉaṟiyēṉ
       ammā poruḷiteṉa aṭaiya viḻuṅkiṉaṇṭi'

   - eṉkiṟār tāyumāṉār.
   cummā irukkum cūḻnilaiyai,
       'kūṭutaluṭaṉ piritalaṟṟu nirttontamāy,
       kuvitaluṭaṉ viritalaṟṟu,
       kuṇamaṟṟu, varaviṉoṭu pōkkaṟṟu,
       nilaiyāṉa kuṟiyaṟṟu malamu maṟṟu,
       nāṭutalu maṟṟu, mēl kīḻ naṭuppakkameṉa
       naṇṇutalu maṟṟu, vantu
       nātamaṟ ṟaivakaip pūtapē tamumaṟṟu,
       ñāturuviṉ ñāṉa maṟṟu,
       vāṭutalu maṟṟu, mēl oṉṟaṟ ṟiraṇṭaṟṟu,
       vākkaṟṟu, maṉamu maṟṟu,
       maṉṉu paripūraṇac cukavāri taṉṉilē
       vāymaṭuttu uṇṭu avacamāyt
       tēṭutalum aṟṟa iṭam ilaiyeṉṟa mauṉiyē!
       cittānta mutti mutalē!
       cirakiri viḷaṅkavaru taṭciṇā mūrttiyē!
       ciṉmayā ṉanta kuruvē!'

   - eṉat tāyumāṉār mēlum aṟivittal iṅku eṇṇattakum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   tēṭit tēṭoṇā tēvaṉai eṉṉuḷē
   tēṭik kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ!
   - appar perumāṉ - cariyai ñāṉamāṉa tiruvaṅka mālai
   talaiyaṭi yāva taṟiyār kāyattil
       talaiyaṭi yucciyil uḷḷatu mūlan
       talaiyaṭi yāṉa aṟivai yaṟintōr
       talaiyaṭi yākavē tāṉirun tārē.

   - tirumantiram 8-2426
   Head and Feet Are Within
   They know not
   The Head and Foot - within body,
   the Head is in Sahasrara (Cranium)
   The Foot is Muladhara;
   Those who visioned thus in the Yogic way,
   Remained in Prayer
   Their heads bowed at Lords feet.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   cāmīpam
   āṉaimukap paramaṉ, iyalpāy aṟiyum aṟivucorupam; āṉmā, aṟivikka aṟiyum
   aṟivucorupam. itaik kurumuka emmāṉ kuṟittatum, āṉmā amalaṉai aṇukum.
   ippēṟu, cāmīpa patam eṉap peṟum. innilai, ciṟanta ñāṉattiṟ kiriyai
   mikkavarkkuc cittikkum. nam auvaip pāṭṭiyār, ataik kūṟum muṟaiyē kōṭi
   peṟum.
   55. eṉṉai aṟivittu eṉakkaruḷ ceytu
   patavurai:
   eṉṉai aṟivittu - āṉmāvākiya eṉatu nilaiyai aṟiyac ceytu,
   eṉakku aruḷ ceytu - eṉakku cāmīpa patattai vaḻaṅki (eṉṟavāṟu).
   ulakil aṟivuṭaiya poruḷkaḷ pala; apporuḷkaṭku iṟaivaṉ āṇṭāṉ;
   avai aṭimaikaḷ eṉappeṟum. aṟivilāta poruḷkaḷum pala; uriya avaikaḷai
   uṭaiyāṉ vimalaṉ. eṉavē, ipporuḷkaḷ emmāṉ uṭaimai.
   āṉaimukap paramaṉ kurumukamāki vantu aṟivittāltāṉ, āṉmā ivaikaḷai aṟiya
   iyalum. pattu nāṭikaḷaiyum tūymai ceytu, mūlakkaṉalai mūccuk kāṟṟāl
   mēleḻuppi, mati maṇṭala amutai mānti, cila kālam irukkum uṭalaip
   palakālam nīṭikkac ceyyum kaṉatta citti kaivantālum, ñāṉam peṟāviṭil
   uyti kāṭṭum uyarkati nērā. itu tiṇṇam. ataṉāṟṟāṉ, 'eṉṉai yaṟivittu
   eṉakkaruḷ ceytu' eṉṟu koñcik kūṟukiṟār kuṇavatiyār.
   kavaṉattil uḷḷa katai:
   maṉṉaṉ makaṉ oruvaṉai maṟavar kavarntaṉar. avaṉ iyalpai avaṉ
   aṟiyātapaṭi,
   vāḻumāṟu avaṉai vaḷarttaṉar. kālam pala kaṭantu, paripākam eytiṉāṉ
   pālaṉ.
   naṇṇiyatu nallūḻ.
   taraṇipaṉ orunāḷ taṉaiyaṉai aṟintāṉ. āyiṉum, taṉaiyaṉ tantaiyai
   aṟintilaṉ.
   'maṟavar makaṉ allaṉ nī! eṉ maintaṉ. kavarntaṉar kāṉavar; vaḷarttavar
   paṇpu vāyttilai. uṉakkē aracu uriyatu. ūṉṟip pār!' eṉṟu iṉṉapala
   ētukkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, taṉaiyaṉait taṉṉavaṉ ākkiṉaṉ taraṇipaṉ eṉṟoru
   varalāṟu, palarum aṟintatu.
   atupōl, aimpula vēṭar cūḻalil akappaṭṭu, ariya taṉ iyalpai
   aṟiyātiruntatu āṉmā. kaṭṭaṟukka vantatu kālam. annāḷil, kaṇmuṉ
   kuruvaṭivil kāṭciyāyiṉar kaṇēcar.
   'kēḷ! pulaṉ nukar iṉpam uṉakkaṉṟu. pēriṉpap pēṟṟiṟku urimai uṭaiyavaḷ
   nī!'
   - iṅṅaṉam tiruvaruḷil tōya vaikkum uriya meyyuṇarvāl uṇartti, taṉ
   pēriṉpac celvattaip peṟa vaittaṉar eṉpatu veḷippaṭa,
   'eṉṉai yaṟivittu eṉakkaruḷ ceytu'
       eṉṟa aṭi eṇṇiyeṇṇi iṉpuṟaṟku uriyatu.
       'taṉṉai yaṟivatu aṟivām, a·taṉṟip
       piṉṉai yaṟivatu pēyaṟi vāmē'

   - eṉṟu tirumantiram itaṟkāṉa marapu kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvupatēcattiṉ piṉ,
   peṟṟavar ivar eṉum paṟṟutal illai; uṟṟavar eṉṟu evaraiyum toṟṟutal
   illai. mutalvaṉ ceyta upatēcattāl, muṉṉilaic cuṭṭoḻiyum eṉpatu kuṟippu.
   'pārāti pūtam nī yallai - uṉṉip
       pār intiriyam karaṇam nī yallai
       ārāy uṇarvunī eṉṟāṉ - aiyaṉ
       aṉpāy uraitta col āṉantam tōḻī!'
       'aṉṟeṉṟum āmeṉṟum uṇṭō - uṉakku
       āṉantam vēṇṭiṉ aṟivākic caṟṟē
       niṉṟāl teriyum eṉavē - maṟai
       nītiyem āti nikaḻttiṉāṉ tōḻī!
       caṅkara caṅkara campu'

   - eṉat tāyumāṉār, mauṉakuru tamakkuc ceyta upatēcattai uraittal iṅku
   uṇarat takum.
   'aintu pulaṉ aimpūtam karaṇamāti
       aṭutta kuṇam attaṉaiyum allai yallai;
       inta uṭal aṟivu aṟiyāmaiyum nī yallai;
       yātoṉṟu paṟṟiṉ ataṉ iyalpāy niṉṟu,
       pantamaṟum paḷiṅkaṉaiya cittunī; uṉ
       pakkuvam kaṇṭu aṟivikkum pāṉmaiyēm yām'

   - eṉṟu tāyumāṉārkku mauṉakuru pōtitta pāṭalum iṅku aṟiyat takum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   'ūṉil uyirppai oṭukki oṇcuṭar
       ñāṉa viḷakkiṉai yēṟṟi naṉpulattu
       ēṉai vaḻitiṟan(tu) ēttuvārk(ku) iṭar
       āṉa keṭuppaṉa añce ḻuttumē!'

   - tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ - kiriyai ñāṉam cuṭṭum pañcākkarap patikap
   pāṭal.
   kāṉuṟu kōṭi kaṭikamaḻ cantaṉam
       vāṉuṟu māmala riṭṭu vaṇaṅkiṉum
       ūṉiṉai nīkki uṇarpavark kallatu
       tēṉamar pūṅkaḻal cēravoṇ ṇātē.

   - tirumantiram - 5-1452 - kiriyai ñāṉam
   True Worship is Worship Within
   You may adore Him with sandal fragrance exceeding,
   That grows on peaks atop in forests interior;
   You may worship Him with flowers rare,
   That bloom in Heaven's gardens;
   Unless you shed your fleshy attachments
   And realize Him in the depths of your heart
   You shall never never reach his Holy Feet
   That is like flowers that shed honey dew.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   viṉai mutal
   aṟam, pāvam iraṇṭaṟkum mutaṟkāraṇam mūlaviṉai. ivviṉai, mummalattuḷ
   oṉṟu. mūlaviṉai, ceyalāka veḷippaṭiṉ aṉṟip payaṉ ceyal illai. ataṉāl
   ataṉai, iruviṉaiyiṉ vēṟāka eṇṇuvatu illai. am mūlaviṉaiyaik kāmiya
   malam eṉpar. avviṉai, anāti toṭṭu āṉmāvil aṭarntu uḷḷatu. pīṭaḻikkum
   puṇṇiya pāvac ceyalkaḷ, ataṉiliruntu piṟantaṉa. avaikaḷiṉ payaṉ iṉpam
   tuṉpam. aṉupavitta piṉ avaikaḷ aḻitalāl avviṉaikkum āti antam uṇṭu.
   karumalattoṭu kalappuṟṟu māyā vaṭivukaḷil maṉṉi, kāraṇam kāṭṭāmal
   kaṭṭuppaṭutti, ōyāmal palvēṟu uṭal tantu, āṉmāviṟkup pulaṉākāmal,
   amalaṉ āṇaiyiṉ aṭaṅki iyaṅkum ikkāmiyam. ataṉ vēraiyē kalli eṟintaṉar
   nam kaṇapati perumāṉ eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār pāṭṭiyār. paṭittup pāruṅkaḷ anta
   ōr aṭiyai!
   56. muṉṉai viṉaiyiṉ mutalaik kaḷaintu
   patavurai:
   muṉṉai - anāti kālam toṭṭuvarum,
   viṉaiyiṉ - (piṟappukaṭkuk kāraṇamāṉa) iruviṉaikaḷiṉ,
   mutalai - vērāṉa kaṉma malattai,
   kaḷaintu - vēroṭu kalli eṟintu (eṉṟavāṟu).
   teḷimaṉam
   pācañāṉattāl piṟakkum karuvi aṟiviṟku, vākku eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkum. pacu
   ñāṉamāṉa āṉma aṟivai, maṉmaṉam eṉpatu marapu. vīṭṭiṉpam tara, tūya
   māyaiyil iruntu maṉmaṉam tōṉṟum. maṉmaṉattil oṭuṅkutal, maṉōlayam
   eṉappeṟum. kaviṉuṭaiya nākku cuvaiyiṉpam kāṇpatupōl, maṉmaṉam, ariya
   pēriṉpam peṟṟu ataṉ mayamākum.
   ulakam nilaiyilātatu; tiruvaruḷ oṉṟē mey. ulakaic cārntu ulaka mayamāy,
   aruḷai aṭaintu aruḷmayamākum āṉmā. itaṉaik kurumukamāyiruntu teyvamē
   kuṟippiṭṭu uṇarttum. atai aṉupavattil aṟintatum teḷivaṭaiyum cittam.
   malaviruḷ kalavāmal, aruḷ oḷi niṟainta tūya cintaiyē teḷinta mayamāyt
   tērvu peṟṟirukkum. uttamap pāṭṭi, innuṭpattai ōtum aḻakē aḻaku.
   57. vākkum maṉamum illā maṉōlayam
   58. tēkkiyē eṉṟaṉ cintai teḷivittu
   patavurai:
   vākkum - vāyāl pēcutalum,
   maṉamum - maṉattāl niṉaittalum,
   illā - illāta,
   maṉōlayam - maṉanilai oḻukkam,
   tēkkiyē - niṟaintu perukac ceytu,
   eṉṟaṉ cintai teḷivittu - (ataṉāl) eṉatu maṉmaṉattait teḷivuṟac ceytu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   eṉṉa aṟputamāṉa ceyti!
   'maṉmaṉam eṅkuṇṭu vāyuvum aṅkuṇṭu
       maṉmaṉam eṅkillai vāyuvum aṅkillai
       maṉmaṉat tuḷḷē makiḻntirup pōrkku
       maṉmaṉat tuḷḷē maṉōlayam āmē'

   - eṉṟu tirumūlarum,
   'vākkum maṉamum iṟanta poruḷ kāṇil
   ākkaikku aḻivillai yām'
   - eṉum auvai kuṟaḷum, arumaiyāka itaṉai aṟivittal iṅku aṟiyattakum.
   'vāḻtta vāyum niṉaikka maṭaneñcum tāḻtta ceṉṉiyum' iṟaivaṉ tantuḷaṉ;
   maṉattiṉ tūṇṭutalāl, kāyam paṇiyiṟ kātal koḷum. vāyum taṉ paṇiyāṉa
   vāḻttutalaic ceyyum. kāyavuṇarvu, varavarak karuttil aṭaṅkum. vākkiṉ
   uṇarvum maṉattil oṭuṅkum. kaṭavuṭ kātal koṇṭa maṉam, iṟutiyil taṉṉaiyē
   tuṟakkum. itutāṉ 'maṉōlayam' eṉappeṟum. avvamayam, pirukirutiyiṉ
   pōrvaiyē ivvulakam eṉṉum teḷivu cintaiyil tikaḻum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   tiruppukaḻ - peruttavacaṉa vakuppil varum 'urukku tiruvaruḷ tiḷaittu
   makiḻtara uḷattoṭu urai ceyal oḷittu viṭuvatum' eṉṟa pakutikku, aṭiyēṉ
   eḻutiya urai iṅku poruntak kāṇpōm:
   aruṇakiriyāriṉ vāḻvil murukaṉ nikaḻttiya aṟputaṅkaḷ pala. aṇṇāmalaiyār
   ālayattiṉ kōpurattiliruntu vīḻnta aruṇakiriyārait tāṅkiyaruḷiya murukaṉ
   ceyta upatēcam, 'cummā iru collaṟa!' eṉpatē.
   kantaranupūtiyil ataip pāṭukiṟār aruṇakiriyār:
   cemmāṉ makaḷait tiruṭun tiruṭaṉ
       pemmāṉ murukaṉ piṟavā ṉiṟavāṉ
       cummā irucol laṟaveṉ ṟalumē
       ammā poru ḷoṉṟu maṟintilaṉē!

   collaṟac cummā iruttal yōkacamāti. pēcciṉṟic cummāyiruttal vīṇ. iṅku
   col aṟuttal eṉpatu pēccum, cintaiyum niṟuttuvatu.
   vākku maṉamum iraṇṭu mavuṉamām
       vākku mavuṉattu vantālu mūṅkaiyām
       vākku maṉamum mavuṉamāñ cuttarē
       ākkumac cuttattai yāraṟi vārkaḷē!

   tirumantiram - tantiram - 7 - 1896
   Mauna is Stillness of Both Thought and Speech
   To attain stillness of Speech and Thought at once is mauna
   Mauna sans Speech alone
   Is but state of dumbness;
   Only when Speech and Thought are alike in mauna
   Are you in State Suddha (Perfection)
   Who but knows
   That Suddha state to bring about?
   vākku maṉamu maṟainta maṟaipporuḷ
       nōkkumiṉ nōkkap paṭumporuḷ nuṇṇitu
       pōkkoṉṟum illai varavillai kēṭillai
       ākkamum attaṉai āyntu koḷ vārkkē.

   tirumantiram - tantiram - 9 - 2854
   He is Subtle Beyond Thought and Speech-Seek and Find Him
   Beyond speech and thought
   Is hidden that Vedic Object;
   Look at it;
   It is an Object subtle by far;
   It has no coming, nor going, no perishing;
   All blessings are
   For those who seek Lord true.
   tiruvaruḷ tiḷaippu eṉpatu vākkum maṉamum illā maṉōlayattil nikaḻvatu.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   cārūpam
   kaṇkaḷ iruḷaik kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. atē kaṇkaḷ oḷiyaiyum aṟikiṉṟaṉa; atupōl,
   aṟiyāmai mayamāṉa akam, aṟivumayam ākum. vikaṟpamāṉa inta irunilai
   vilaka vēṇṭum.
   tattuvaṅkaḷiṉ nuṭpattai aṟiyum poḻutu, oḷivaḷar ñāṉam utayam ākum. anta
   ‘aṟikiṟōm itaṉai’ eṉum cintaṉaiyum akaṉṟatāyiṉ, āṉantamayamākum āṉmā.
   'iruḷatu cakti, veḷiyatu em aṇṇal' eṉat tirumantiram terivittapaṭi,
   teḷinta iṭattil teyva iṉpam. eṉṉa arumai! iraṇṭē aṭikaḷil evvaḷavu
   periya ceyti! pāruṅkaḷ pāṭṭi kāṭṭum pāṭṭai!
   59. iruḷveḷi iraṇṭuk(ku) oṉṟiṭam eṉṉa
   60. aruḷtarum āṉantat(tu) aḻuttieṉ ceviyil
   patavurai:
   iruḷ - iruḷaic cārnta pōtum,
   veḷi - oḷiyaic cārnta pōtum,
   iraṇṭukku - anta iraṇṭaṟkum,
   oṉṟu iṭam eṉṉa - kaṇṇē iṭamāṉāṟpōla,
   eṉ ceviyil - (ulakac cārpāl ulakamayam; civaccārpāl
   civamayam āyiṉai eṉṟu) aṭiyēṉ ceviyil,
   aruḷtarum - tiruvaruṭ pēṟṟait tarum,
   āṉantattu aḻutti - iṉpac ceytiyai aḻunta upatēcittu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   'oḷiyiruḷ kaṇṭakaṇ pōla vēṟāy uḷ
   oḷiyiruḷ nīṅka uyarcivam āmē'
   - eṉpatu tirumūlar kuṟippu.
   i·tu aruḷiṉ corupanilai. iṅku āṉmā kaṇapati aruḷil kalantu aṭaṅkum;
   cārūpakati eṉṟu itaṉaic cāṟṟuvar periyōr.
   aruḷē iṟaivar tirumēṉi. aruḷaic cārtal iṟaivar ākattu utittal
   eṉappeṟum.
   'ivvuṭampu nīṅkum muṉṉē entāy kēḷ! niṉṉaruḷām
   avvuṭampuk kuḷḷē avatarikkak kāṇpēṉō'
   - eṉṟu mēlum mēlum vēṇṭuvar mēlōr.
   'tattuvam āṉavaṟṟiṉ taṉmaikaḷ uṇaruṅkālai,
       uyntu uṇarntiṭa utippatu oḷivaḷar ñāṉam ākum;
       attaṉmai aṟiyum āṟum akaṉṟiṭa atuvāy āṉmāc
       cuttama tāṉa ñāṉat(tu) orumutal tōṉṟu maṉṟē'

   - eṉum civappirakācam, itaṉai evvaḷavu naṉṟāka uṇarntuḷatu eṉpatu
   yūkikkat takuvatu. itu, puḻuvait taṉṉiṟamākkum kuḷaviyiṉ ceytipōl
   irukkiṟatallavā!
   aṟiyāmai aṟivu eṉum iraṇṭaṟkum piṟappiṭam oṉṟē eṉumpaṭi, perukiya
   tiruvaruḷāl uṇṭākum pēriṉpak kaṭalil, mūḻkac ceytāṉ mutalvaṉ eṉa
   ivvaṭikkup poruḷ koḷḷiṉumām.
   'aṟiyāmai aṟivāti pirivāka aṟivārkaḷ
       aṟivāka niṉṟa nilaiyil
       cintaiyaṟa nileṉṟu cummā vuṇarttimēl
       ciṉmayā ṉanta veḷḷam
       tēkkit tiḷaittunāṉ atuvā yirukkanī
       ceycitram mika naṉṟukāṇ'

   - eṉum takavu niṟainta tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ, iṅku nam niṉaiviṟku varukiṟār
   allavā!
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   irāppakal aṟṟa iṭattē iruntu
       parākkaṟa āṉantat tēṟal parukār
       irāppakal aṟṟa iṟaiyaṭi iṉpattu
       irāppakal māyai iraṇṭiṭat tēṉē.

   - tirumantiram - 1-331 - kaḷḷuṇṇāmai
   Joys of Sivananda Nectar
   In meditation lost, 'rid of the cycle of Night and Day',
   Dead to outward things, they drink not the bliss-nectar -
   The Bliss of the Lord's Feet that neither night nor day knoweth,
   I, caught in Maya causing Night and Day, struggle to reach.
   taṅkiya cārūpan tāṉeṭṭām yōkamān
       taṅkuñ caṉmārkkan taṉilaṉṟik kaikūṭā
       aṅkat tuṭalcitti cātaṉa rākuvar
       iṅkiva rāka viḻivaṟṟa yōkamē.

   - tirumantiram - 5-1510 - cārūpam
   Only Jnana-in-Yoga Leads to Sarupa State
   The State of Sarupa is, no doubt, reached
   Through the eight-fold yoga way;
   But unless it be Sanmarga-in-Yoga,
   The Sarupa state cannot be;
   The Yoga way but leads to bodily Siddhis diverse;
   But for the Sarupa state to realize,
   None these but the pure way of Jnana-in-Yoga.
   oḷiyum iruḷum orukālun tīrā
       oḷiyuḷōrk kaṉṟō oḻiyā toḷiyum
       oḷiyiruḷ kaṇṭakaṇ pōlavē ṟāyuḷ
       oḷiyiruḷ nīṅka vuyirciva māmē.

   - tirumantiram -7-1819 - aruḷoḷi
   By Inner Light Unite One in Sivam
   Interminable are light and darkness
   Only to those who have light, will darkness cease;
   To the eyes that see light, darkness is not;
   So, too, when the inner light dispels darkness,
   The Jiva with Siva one becomes.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   kalaiilaṅ kummaḻu kaṭṭaṅkam kaṇṭikai kuṇṭalam
       vilaiilaṅ kummaṇi māṭattar vīḻimiḻalaiyār
       talaiilaṅ kumpiṟai tāḻvaṭam cūlam tamarukam
       alaiilaṅ kumpuṉal ēṟṟavark kumaṭi yārkkumē.
       - tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ, patamuttiyiṉ
       uccamāy eytum cārūpanilaiyaic cuṭṭum ariya

   (tiruvīḻimiḻalaip patikattiṉ) pāṭalitu.
   (http://www.tamil.net/projectmadurai/pub/pm0173/tevaram3a.html#dt3009)
   paramutti nilai
   maṉmaṉamum maṟaiyum; kāṇpāṉ, kāṭci, kāṇappaṭu poruḷum karakkum. āṉmā
   kaṇapatiyoṭu attuvitamāka aṭaṅkum. catteṉum meyyoḷi, citteṉum aṟivoḷi,
   āṉantameṉum iṉpa oḷi mūṉṟum, oṉṟaṟku oṉṟu viyāpaka viyāppiyam ākiviṭum.
   ituvē paramāṉantap pēṟu.
   61. ellai yillā ānantam aḷittu
   patavurai:
   ellai illā - varampu illāta,
   ānantam aḷittu - cāyucciya iṉpa uṇarvai aruḷi
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   aruḷ neṟi
   karuviyil aṭaṅkak karuvikaḷ tōṉṟum; iruḷuṭaṉ, iṉpam tuṉpam iraṇṭum
   aḷikkum nilaiyatu innilai. ammavō amma! eṉṉa paripavam!
   aruḷil aṭaṅka aruḷ tōṉṟum; itu iṉpamēyāṉa iyalnilai. iṅku, ōm eṉkiṟatu
   nam uṇarvu.
   kavarimāṉ iyalpāṉa kastūri maṇam vīcum; atupōl, innilaiyil,
   antakkaraṇattuḷ civamaṇam vīcum. maḻaiyiruḷil ticai teriyāmal uḻalpavar
   cūriya utayam kāṇpatupōl, ticai tappiya kappal tappit tuṟai cērak
   kāṇpatu pōl, viṭācciṟaiyarkku viṭutalai kiṭaittatu pōl, akalā nōyarkku
   annōy akaṉṟatupōl, tiruvaruḷ neṟi kaṇṭavarkkut tuṉpam ellām tīrum; inta
   anupavamum kāṭṭiṉaṉ āṉaimukap paramaṉ eṉkiṟār nam auvaip pirāṭṭiyār.
   62. allal kaḷaintē aruḷvaḻi kāṭṭi
   patavurai:
   aruḷvaḻi kāṭṭi - aruḷneṟi eṉum ñāṉamārkkattaik kāṭṭi,
   allal kaḷaintu - (kāṭṭiya aḷavil) piṟavit tuyarai vēraṟuttu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   allal kaḷaintu aruḷvaḻi kāṭṭutalai,
   'pārāti yaṇṭaṅkaḷ attaṉaiyum vaikkiṉṟa
       paraveḷiyiṉ uṇmai kāṭṭi,
       paṟṟumaṉa veḷikāṭṭi, maṉaveḷiyiṉil tōynta
       pāviyēṉ paricu kāṭṭi,
       tārāḷamāy niṟka niṟcintai kāṭṭic
       catākāla niṭṭai yeṉavē
       cakacanilai kāṭṭiṉai, cukātīta nilayan
       taṉaikkāṭṭa nāḷ cellumō?
       kārāra eṇṇarum ananta kōṭikaḷ niṉṟu
       kālūṉṟi maḻaipoḻital pōl
       kālvīci miṉṉip paṭarntu paraveḷiyelām
       kammi ānanta veḷḷam
       cōrātu poḻiyavē karuṇaiyiṉ muḻaṅkiyē
       toṇṭaraik kūvum mukilē!
       cuttanirk kuṇamāṉa parateyvamē! parañ
       cōtiyē! cukavāriyē!'

   - eṉa varum tāyumāṉār pāṭal, kāṇa iyalāta aruḷvaḻiyai āṉmāviṟkuk kāṭṭum
   karuṇaiyai veḷip paṭuttukiṉṟatu allavā! annilaiyaittāṉ nam auvaiyārum,
   allal kaḷaintu aruḷvaḻi kāṭṭiṉāṉ nam aruḷ kuravaṉ eṉkiṟār.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   vāṉ keṭṭu mārutam māyntu
       aḻal nīr maṇ keṭiṉum
       tāṉ keṭṭal iṉṟic
       calippu aṟiyāt taṉmaiyaṉukku
       ūṉ keṭṭu uyirkeṭṭu
       uṇarvu keṭṭu eṉ uḷḷamum pōy
       nāṉ keṭṭavā pāṭit
       teḷḷēṇam koṭṭāmō!

   - māṇikkavācakar - tirutteḷḷēṇam -18
   ñāṉam viḷainteḻu kiṉṟatōr cintaiyuḷ
       ēṉam viḷainteti rēkāṇa vaḻitoṟuṅ
       kūṉal matimaṇ ṭalattetir nīrkaṇṭu
       ūṉam aṟuttuniṉ ṟoṇcuṭa rākumē.

   - tirumantiram - 5-1472 - ñāṉam
   Jnani becomes Light Divine
   In whose thoughts Jnana ripens and swells,
   In his path the Life Boat appears and greets him;
   And thus does he reach the surging waters
   Of the Crescent Moon's sphere.
   And there rid of the impurities,
   He himself the Effulgent Light becomes.
   cāyuc ciyañcāk kiratātītañ cārutal
       cāyuc ciyamupa cāntattut taṅkutaṟ
       cāyuc ciyañciva mātal muṭivilāc
       cāyuc ciyamaṉat tāṉanta cattiyē.

   - tirumantiram - 5-1513 - cāyucciyam
   Sayujyam
   Sayujya is the state of Jagrat-Atita, the
   Beyond Consciousness,
   Sayujya is to abide for ever in Upasantha
   The peace that passes all understanding,
   Sayujya is to become Siva Himself,
   Sayujya is to experience the infinite power of inward bliss,
   Forever and ever.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   cattattiṉ uḷḷē catācivam kāṭṭi..
   aruvuruvam
   turiya meyññāṉa aṟputa meypporuḷ, tayā uruvat tirumēṉi tāṅkum;
   upatēcikkak kurumukamākik kuṟukum. iṭaiyil, aruvamāṉa taṉ iyalai
   aṟivikkum. civam, cakti, vintu, nātam eṉṉum nāṉkum aruvat tirumēṉikaḷ.
   patappaṭum āṉma nilaiyaip pārttu, kaṉinta taṉ aruvuruvaik kāṭṭum;
   aruvuruvattiṉ vēṟu peyar catācivam.
   aintoḻil āṟṟum aruḷ civacakti, cutta māyaiyil uḷḷa catācivattiṉ mūlam,
   akattum puṟattum toḻil āṟṟum. catācivamum, civacaktiyum, aṅkiyum,
   aṅkamumāka eṉṟum amaintuḷar. icceytiyai,
   'aṇṭaṅkaḷ ēḻiṉukku appuṟattu appālum
       uṇṭeṉṟa cakti catācivattu ucci mēl
       kaṇṭam kariyāṉ karuṇai tiruvuruk
       koṇṭaṅku umaikāṇak kūttukan tāṉē'

   - eṉṟu tirumantiram (oṉpatām tantiram - cuntarakkūttu) iṉitu
   iyampukiṉṟatu.
   cāntiyātīta kalaiyil uḷḷatu cattam; a·tu uyirkaḷ uṇarum cūkkuma pūtam.
   ataṟku iṭam, tūla pūtamāṉa ākāyam. ākāya atipar catācivar. āṉma akattum
   puṟattum iruntu aruḷ ceypavar ivar. cāntiyātīta kalaiyaic cārnta
   tattuvaṅkaṭkum, puvaṉaṅkaṭkum ivar talaivar. cīvarkaṭku aruḷal ivartam
   ciṟapput toḻil. eṉiṉum, paṭaittal, kāttal, aḻittal, maṟaittal, aṉaittum
   potuvākac ceyyum puṉita mūrttiyeṉap peṟuvar.
   vāṉ maṇṭala atiparāṉa ivar, āṅkuc cārum āṉmākkaṭkup puṟattil cattamāyp
   polintuḷar.
   cattam eṉpatu cūkkuma vākkiṉ uḷḷaṭaṅkiya ōcai. vittaka inta ōcaiyai
   viḷaivittu, ataṉil catācivar viyāpakamāka viḷaṅkārēl, cāntiyātīta
   kalaiyil uḷḷa uyirkaṭkuc civapōkam utayam ākātu.
   puṟattil iruntu pōkam nukarvippatuṭaṉ, nukar karuviyākavum iruntu,
   akattil cīvarkaḷ yōkam anupavikkap purikiṉṟār.
   civaliṅkam eṉiṉum catācivam eṉiṉum oṉṟē. aṇṭattil cattam; cattattil
   catācivam. piṇṭattil cittam; cittattil civaliṅkam. kāṭṭiṉaṉ itaṉaik
   kaṇapatip perumāṉ. ivaikaḷai uṇarntu uraikkiṉṟār auvaiyār. itaṉai ūṉṟi
   uṇarntu paṭikkumpōtu, namatu uḷḷam pākāy urukukiṟatu.
   63. cattattiṉ uḷḷē catācivam kāṭṭic
   64. cittattiṉ uḷḷē civaliṅkam kāṭṭi
   patavurai:
   cattattiṉ uḷḷē - aṇṭattil nuṇmaip pūtamāṉa cattattait
   toḻilpaṭutti, antac cattattuḷ,
   catācivam kāṭṭi - viyāpaka catācivattai viḷaṅkac ceytu,
   cittattiṉ uḷḷē - (piṇṭattuḷ) uḷḷattuḷ viḷaṅkum,
   civaliṅkam kāṭṭi - civaliṅkattait taricikkac ceytu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   cattamum cittamum attaṉ iruppiṭam eṉalai,
   'cattamum catta maṉamum maṉakkaruttu
   ottaṟi kiṉṟa iṭamum aṟikilar;
   meyttaṟi kiṉṟa iṭam aṟivārkku
   attaṉ iruppiṭam avviṭan tāṉē'
   - eṉum tirumūlar kuṟippai evarum ōti uṇarntuḷar. uyirkaḷ taṉṉai
   aṟintuyya, aruḷāl iṟaivaṉ āṭci naṭattuvaṉ. aruḷvaḻi paṭarum āṉma
   kōṭikaḷ, aṉpum aṟivum eyti, cevviya ceyal pala ceytu, iṉiya
   paṭimuṟaiyēṟi iṉpam eytuvar.
   annilaiyil, cattattil paramaṉ taṉ aruvuruvai aṟivippaṉ. ūṉṟi uṇarntu
   nekiḻvārkku, avar tam cittattil taṉmayamāṉa civaliṅkam kāṭṭuvaṉ.
   puṟattil catācivam; akattil civaliṅkam. ivaikaḷai eṇṇi, āṉantikkum nam
