   akam. tikaḻum puruvanaṭuvil catācivattait tiyāṉippatum, itayattil
   civaliṅka mūrttiyaip pūcippatum marapu.
   'uraiceyum ōcai uraicey pavarkku
       narai tirai yillai namaṉ'
       'ōcaiyiṉ uḷḷē utikkiṉṟa toṉṟuṇṭu
       vācamalar nāṟṟampōl vantu'
       'taṟpara māṉa catācivattō ṭoṉṟil
       uṟṟaṟi villai uyirkku'
       'ōcai uṇarntaṅkē uṇarvaip peṟumparicāl
       īcaṉ karuttāy iru'

   - eṉum auvai kuṟaḷpākkaḷ, iṅku uṇarat takum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   'vāṉattiṉ mītu mayilāṭak kaṇṭēṉ; mayil kuyilāccutaṭi' eṉṟu emes ammā
   pāṭip pirapalamāṉa, vaḷḷalāriṉ varikaḷ. itaṉ tattuva viḷakkam inta
   aṭikkut toṭarpuṭaiyatu.
   iṅkē mayil eṉpatu vintu eṉṟa oḷiyaik kuṟippatu; kuyil nātameṉum oli.
   oliyum oḷiyumē civacakti aikkiyattil āṟu attuvākkaḷāy virintu
   aṇṭaṅkaḷāki niṟpaṉa. caṭkōṇattiṉ āṟu pakkaṅkaḷ cuṭṭuvatum avaṟṟaiyē.
   āṟumukam āṉa poruḷatu. oṭuṅkukaiyil oḷiyeṉum poruḷulakam, oliyil
   aṭaṅkum.
   mīṇṭum oliyiliruntē aṉaittum tōṉṟum. 'vētattoli koṇṭu', 'em iṟai
   nalvīṇai vācikkumē' pōṉṟa tirumuṟaik kuṟippukaḷāl itai uṇaralām.
   'catācivam tattuvam muttamiḻ vētam' eṉṟu tirumūlar cuṭṭuvatu pōl
   cātākkiyat tattuvattiliruntu, akara ukara makaramāṉa ōm eṉum muttamiḻē,
   vēta olikaḷāy virintu perukum. 'nātam eḻunteḻuntōṭi vantuṟaiyum
   tirukkūttu' eṉpār maṇivācakar.
   inta aṇṭa nikaḻvaip piṇṭattil, taharākācattil (vaḷḷal perumāṉ
   cāntōkkiya upaniṭatam cuṭṭum pirammavityaiyām taharavityaiyaip
   pōtittavar) - ciṟṟampalattil kāṇkaiyil - akavāṉattil āṭum mayil,
   kuyilāki oṭuṅkum. akkacci eṉṟu kuṇṭaliṉi mahācaktiyaip pāṭukiṟār;
   potuvāyc cittarkaḷ, vālaippeṇ, kaṇṇammā, kutampāy, ñāṉappeṇṇē eṉṟu
   pāṭuvatu pōla.
   'ātiyām civaṉum avaṉ cōtiyāṉa caktiyuntāṉ aṅkum iṅkum eṅkum uḷavākum'
   eṉṟu pārati pāṭuvatu pōl, civacaktiyē nātavintuvāy, oliyulakāy,
   oḷiyulakāy virintu eṅkum niṟaivaṉa. vintu eṉum oḷi, yōka ucciyil, nātam
   eṉum oliyil aṭaṅkip piṉ nātāntam ākum.
   aṅṅaṉam nātāntam eṭṭa vallōr cittar āvar.
   'cittar civalōkam iṅkē tericittōr
       cattamuñ catta muṭivumtam uḷkoṇṭōr
       nittar nimalar nirāmayar nīḷpara
       muttartam mutti mutalmuppat tāṟē'

   eṉpār tirumūlar. (tirumantiram - 1-125 - upatēcam)
   Siddhas they that Siva's world here visioned,
   Nada and Nadanta deep in them realized,
   The Eternal, the Pure, reposing in Bliss unalloyed,
   Thirty and Six, the steps to Liberation leading.
   mayilaik kuyilākki, nātaṉai nātattil uṇarvatu eppaṭi?
   atai nōkkiya vaḻiyaip pāṭṭiyār paṭippaṭiyāyp pāṭak kēṭṭōm. racapatiyār
   ippakutiyil cuṭṭum oru tirumantirappāṭalil iṉṉum virivāṉa viṭai
   irukkiṟatu.
   itaic cittar paripāṣaiyil 'maṟittēṟṟam' eṉpar. kīḻnōkkiyē celutti
   virayamākum vintuvai, kuṇṭaliṉi yōkattāl kaṭṭi, mēlnōkki maṟittēṟṟum
   cātaṉaiyil puruvamattiyil kaṇ tiṟakkum. itaiyē 'ūrtva rētam vrūpākṣam'
   eṉṟu vētamum cuṭṭum. aṅkē, nilā maṇṭalam eṉum 'mācaṟu tiṅkaḷil' kaṅkai
   ūṟṟeṭuttup perukum.
   ippaṭi vintuvaik kaṭṭi maṟittēṟṟa atu nātattil oṭuṅkum. 'eṉ kulam
   cukkulamtāṉ maintā' eṉṟu pāṭukiṟār cittar kākapucuṇṭar.
   'kulāmrutaika-rasikā' eṉpatu lalitaiyiṉ āyiram (LS 90) nāmaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
   itai 'vintucayam' (Conquest of Bindu) eṉṟu pāṭukiṟār tirumūlar.
   itil mukkiyamāṉa cila pāṭalkaḷaip pārppōm.
   'amutac cacivintu vāmvintu māḷa
       amutap puṉalōṭi aṅkiyiṉ māḷa
       amutac civapōkam ātalāṟ citti
       amutap palāvaṉa māṅkuṟum yōkikkē'

   - tirumantiram - 7-1959 - vintucayam
   Bindu Sublimated by Kundalini Yoga Leads to Ambrosial Flow
   When the Bindu of the body
   Thus perishes (by Yoga)
   It is into Divine Bindu transformed
   Of the ambrosial Lunar Sphere within;
   When this Bindu of the body
   Perishes in the fire of Kundalini,
   The ambrosial waters flow and fill the body;
   Then indeed is Siva Bhoga that is ambrosial sweet;
   And thus bathed in divine waters of ambrosia
   The Yogi attains Siddhis rare.
   vintuvum nātamum viḷaiya viḷaintatu
       vantaip palluyir maṉṉuyi rukkelām
       antamum ātiyu māman tiraṅkaḷum
       vintu aṭaṅka viḷaiyuñ civōkamē.

   Then is SivOham.

   - nirvāṇa ṣaṭakattil āticaṅkarar
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   eṅkumāṉa iṉpa uru
   ulakelām oṭuṅkum nāḷil, eñci niṟpavaṉ iṟaivaṉ. appōtaiya avaṉuṭaiya
   pēruruviṉ muṉ aṇṭamelām aṇuppōl ām. emmāṉ epporuḷiṉum
   taṅkiyirukkumpōtu, aṇuvelām aṇṭamām. itaṉāl, vicuvarūpaṉ, vicuvattiṟku
   antaryāmi, vicuvātikaṉ, vicuvanātaṉ, vicuvēsvaraṉ eṉṟu avaṉai
   ēttukiṉṟatu vētam. itaṉai anupavattil aṟintavarē meyññāṉikaḷ. vaṭivilā
   ākāyam, avartam akakkaṇṇiṟkup pulaṉām. annilaiyil, tēkātma putti tēyntu
   aḻiyum; āhā! aḷaviṟantu perukum āṉantam. itaṉai 'iṉpa racattē parukip
   palakālum, eṉṟaṉuyirk(ku) ātaravuṟ ṟaruḷvāyē!' eṉkiṟatu teyvac cantat
   tiruppukaḻ.
   65. aṇuviṟ kaṇuvāy appāluk kappālāyk
   66. kaṇumuṟṟi niṉṟa karumpuḷḷē kāṭṭi
   patavurai:
   aṇuviṟku aṇuvāy - aṇuviṟkuḷ (atinuṭpa) aṇuvākiyum,
   appālukku appālāy - aṇṭamelām kaṭantu peritākip paravi virintu,
   kaṇumuṟṟi niṉṟa - kaṇukkaḷ mutirntu nalamuṟa viḷainta,
   karumpuḷḷē kāṭṭi - karumpiṉ iṉpa nalattai eṉṉuḷ
   attuvitamāka iruntu uṇarac ceytu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   tām peṟṟa iṉpattaip piṟarkku aṟivippar āṉṟōr. aṟivikkum amayam, ulakam
   aṟiporuḷāṉa karumpu, kaṉi, pāl, tēṉ mutaliya cuvaip poruḷkaḷai,
   makattāṉa uvamaiyākak kaiyāḷvatu marapu. avvaḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟi,
   'karumpuḷḷē kāṭṭi' eṉṟu pāṭik kāṭṭukiṟār nam pāṭṭiyār.
   'aṇṭamōr aṇuvām perumaikoṇ ṭaṇuvōr
   aṇṭamām ciṟumaikoṇ ṭaṭiyēṉ
   uṇṭaūṇ uṉakkām vakaieṉa(tu) uḷḷat(tu)
   uḷkalan teḻuparañ cōti'
   eṉṟu teyvak karuvūrt tēvarum,
   'aṇuvuḷ avaṉum avaṉuḷ aṇuvum
       kaṇuvaṟa niṉṟa kalappa tuṇarār'
       eṉṟu tantira corupamāṉa tirumantiramum,
       'paṉaittaṭakkai entai maṟṟi yāvuḷa
       cintai ceyyum tēvatai namakkē'
       'ēyaniṉ taṉṉai yallāl vēṟutaḻīit toḻumō
       vaṇaṅkāta viyaṉciramē'

   - eṉa varum mūtta piḷḷaiyār mummaṇik kōvaiyum,
   'kaṉiyiṉum kaṭṭi paṭṭa karumpiṉum
       iṉiyaṉ taṉṉaṭain tārkkiṭai marutaṉē'

   - eṉa aruntamiḻ vēta apparum aruḷiya aruḷmoḻikaḷ iṅku nam niṉaivil ūṟi
   neñcattai nekiḻvikkiṉṟaṉa allavā!

   - tirumantiram - 8-2297 - aṇṭāti pētam

   One paltry planet, a few thousand years - hardly worth the attention of

   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   vēṭamum nīṟum
   tavam niṟai oruvarai, makattāṉavar eṉṟu ulakam matikkum. ataṟku ayalāyp
   paraṉai maṟantu, ūṉuṭal vaḷarttu uyirkkum evarkkum, nāṉē teyvam eṉṟu
   namaṉ varuvāṉ.
   aruntavam muyaṉṟu, paramuttiyaip peṟuvar immaiyil periyōrkaḷ. mummalak
   kaṭṭu nīṅkiṉareṉiṉum, pirārattuvattāl vanta uṭal vilakum varai, viṉai
   ivarkaḷai viṭṭu vilakuvatillai.
   niṭṭaiyil iruntu nīṅkiya nēram, pirārattuvam, ciṟcila camayam tāṉē
   avarkaḷait tākkum. ataṉāl viruppum veṟuppum, tattuvak kāṭciyum,
   viparīta uṇarvum viḷaivatu iyalpu. avai nikaḻā vaṇṇam, 'yāṉai
   mukattāṉaik kātalāl kūppuvartam kai'. ataṉuṭaṉ, kaṇapati aṭiyavarai,
   apperumāṉ corupamākavē ārātippar. avaruṭaṉ kūṭuvar. ainteḻuttai ōtuvar.
   ñāṉa nūl āyvar. aṭiyavar uṟavu mikumpaṭi, mūlap paramporuḷ tirumuṉ
   muṟaiyiṭṭē niṟpar.
   tiruvaṭip pēṟṟaip peṟṟārtam perumaiyaip peritu pēcuvar. aṭiyavarkku
   aṭiyam yām eṉṟu ārvattoṭu aṟivippar; avarkaṭku āṉa paṇi attaṉaiyum
   ceyvar. innilaiyaraic cīvaṉmuttar eṉṟu civākamaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   tuṇaiviyiṭattil oruvaṉ koṇṭirukkum aṉpai, avaḷ uṟaviṉariṭattu avaṉ
   kāṭṭum aṉpaik kaṇṭu kaṇippar. atupōl iṟaivaṉiṭam vaitta aṉpai, avaṉ
   aṭiyariṭam kāṭṭum aṉpaik kaṇṭu kaṇippatu paṇṭai marapu.
   'īcaṉukku aṉpillār, aṭiyavarkku aṉpillār,
       evvuyirkkum aṉpillār, tamakkum aṉpillār,
       pēcuvatu eṉ aṟivillāp piṇaṅkaḷainām iṇaṅkil
       piṟappiṉilum iṟappiṉilum piṇaṅkiṭuvar viṭu nī
       ācaiyoṭum araṉ aṭiyār aṭiyārai aṭaintiṭṭu
       avarkarumam uṉ karumam ākac ceytu
       kūci moḻin(tu) aruḷ ñāṉak kuṟiyil niṉṟu
       kumpiṭṭut taṭṭamiṭṭuk kūttāṭit tiriyē!'

   - eṉum civañāṉa cittiyār, iṅku nam cintaṉaiyil ūṟip peruki
   uṟavāṭukiṟatu allavā!
   vēṭamum nīṟum ulakil viḷaṅka nilaittāl, kūṭum toṇṭarum avartam uṟavum
   koḷḷa varum.

       aṭiyārkku iṉiyaṉ'

   - eṉak kapilar, kaṇapatiyaik kuṟippiṭuvatum, iṅkuk karutat takum.
   iṟaivaṉai meyyaṉpuṭaṉ vāḻttuvārum, vaṇaṅkuvārum, tēvar mutaliya
   evarālum vāḻttap peṟṟum, vaṇaṅkap peṟṟum perumai peṟuvar.
   'toḻappaṭum tēvar toḻappaṭuvāṉait toḻuta piṉṉai
       toḻappaṭum tēvartammāl toḻuvikkum tam toṇṭaraiyē'

   - eṉum appar tēvāram iṅku karutat takum.
   pacu malattai nīṟṟi eṭuttatu nīṟu; atu, pacupōtaṅ keṭuttaṟku
   aṟikuṟiyāka aṟiyap peṟum; parācakti vipūtimayam eṉkiṟatu tirunīṟṟup
   patikam. kaṇṇutaliṉ kaṇkaḷil iruntoḻukiya aruḷtuḷikaḷ, kaṇmaṇikaḷ
   eṉappeṟum. nīṟṟaiyum, anta kaṇmaṇi mālaikaḷaiyum aṇivar aṭiyārkaḷ.
   puruvanaṭu tiyāṉa iṭam. aṅku corupam āvirppavikkum. atai niṉaivuṟutta,
   cantaṉam, kuṅkumam neṟṟiyil iṭuvar. neṟṟi turiya veḷiyiṭam. aṅku
   veḷippaṭum aruḷ veḷiyai eṇṇaṟku aṟikuṟiyākavum tirunīṟu tarippatu
   marapu. kaṉmam ulakiyalil uṟavāṭa viṭum; tiruvaruḷ aṭiyariṭam uṟavu
   pūṇa utavum; auvaiyārkku atu ceytāṉām attaṉ; pāṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār
   pāruṅkaḷ!
   67. vēṭamum nīṟum viḷaṅka niṟuttik
   68. kūṭumeyt toṇṭar kuḻāttuṭaṉ kūṭṭi
   patavurai:
   vēṭamum - (kaṇṭikai taritta aṭiyavar) tiruvēṭamum,
   nīṟum - tirunīṟum,
   viḷaṅka niṟutti - ulakil viḷaṅki nilaipeṟac ceytu,
   kūṭum - oṉṟukūṭi iṉpuṟum,
   meyttoṇṭar - meyyaṭiyār
   kuḻāttuṭaṉ kūṭṭi - kūṭṭattil eṉaiyum oruttiyākak kūṭṭuvittu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   meyññāṉattāl malattaṉmai akaṉṟu, veḷḷiya aruḷoḷi niṟainta
   civattaṉmaiyai aṭaitalai uṇarttukiṉṟatu tirunīṟu eṉalum oruvakai.
   tirunīṟṟilum, vittaka nīṟaṇinta vēṭattilum, tayiril neypōl taṟparaṉ
   taṅkiyuḷaṉ. piṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, pālil neypōl paṭarntuḷaṉ; aṅṅaṉam amaintu,
   avaikaḷiṉ corupam viḷaṅka, paripākattiṟkut takkapaṭi, annuṭpaṅkaḷai
   uyirkaṭku aṟivittu uyti tarutalai mutalaṭi kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. cārntataṉ
   vaṇṇamāka aṭiyavar uṟavu cārac ceykiṉṟa caturar kaṇapati. itaic ciṟitu
   virivāka āyvam. ataṟkuriya payaṉum viḷaiyum.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   kaṅkāḷaṉ pūcuṅ kavacat tirunīṟṟai
       maṅkāmaṟ pūci makiḻvarē yāmākil
       taṅkā viṉaikaḷuñ cāruñ civakati
       ciṅkāra māṉa tiruvaṭi cērvarē.

   - tirumantiram - 6-1666 - tirunīṟu
   Power of Holy Ashes
   The Sacred ashes of Siva
   Who has bones for His garland
   Are an armour indeed impregnable;
   For them who in joy smear it
   Karmas take flight,
   And Siva-state comes seeking;
   And they shall reach His handsome Feet.

   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   vēṭap polivu
   kāmikam mutal vātuḷam īṟāka, irukkum ākamaṅkaḷ irupatteṭṭu. avaikaḷ,
   catācivattiṉ vimala aintu tirumukaṅkaḷil iruntu veḷi vantaṉa eṉpar
   mēlōr.
   iṉpa nīṟu tarikkum iṭaṅkaḷ ivai, anta iṭaṅkaṭku uriya aḷavukaḷ ivai,
   vāḻa aṇiyum vakai ivai eṉṉum ariya nuṭpaṅkaḷ, ākamaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam aṟiyap
   peṟum.
   neṟṟi, tōḷ, mārpu, ucci, kai, muḻaṅkāl mutaliyaṉa, iṉiya tirunīṟu
   tarikkum iṭaṅkaḷ.
   neṟṟiyilum, tōḷkaḷilum, mārpilum aḷavu āṟu aṅkulam. ucci, kai, muḻaṅkāl
   mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavu ōr aṅkulam.
   tiripuṇṭaramāka aṇital, uttūḷaṉamākap pūcutal iraṇṭum uṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam
   vititta iyalpil, vititta kālaṅkaḷil, nīṟu tarippar nimalaṉ aṭiyar.
   'puṇṇiyar pūcum veṇṇīṟu' eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟai. 'nīṟillā neṟṟi pāḻ'
   eṉpatu auvai pōtam.
   aḻaku karutiyum, tūymaiyai niṉaintum, tiruvaruḷ pēṟṟaic cintittum
   nimala nīṟu pūcuvatu niyati. puṟat tūymaiyai veṇmaiyāṉa tirunīṟum,
   akattūymaiyai aṉpiṉ japamum aṟivikkum.
   'pūcum nīṟupōl uḷḷum puṉitarkaḷ' eṉṟu pukalkiṟatu purāṇam. vipūti,
   uruttirākkam aṇinta kōlam, tiruvēṭam eṉappeṟum.
   'koṇṭa vēṭam, araṉ aṭiyar vēṭam, itu
   kuṟaivi lātatava vēṭam' eṉavum,
   'arantai tīrkkum aṭiyavar mēṉimēl
       niranta nīṟṟoḷiyāl, nirai tūymaiyāl,
       puranta ainteḻut(tu) ōcai politalāl,
       paranta āyiram pāṟkaṭal pōlvatu'

   -eṉavum varum tiruttoṇṭar purāṇam, tiruvēṭap polivai
   niṉaivuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
   tirunīṟṟup patikam, pañcākkarap patikam mutaliyaṉa, avaikaḷiṉ arumaiyai
   uṇarntu, mantira marapil camayak kuravarkaḷ vāy malarntavai.
   'nīṟu puṉaivār viṉaiyai nīṟu ceyta lālē
       vīṟu taṉi nāmamatu nīṟeṉa viḷaṅkum
       cīṟu narakat(tu) uyir celāvakai maruntāk
       kūṟuṭaiya tēvi kaiyil muṉṉiṟai koṭuttāṉ'

   - eṉum āṉṟōr aruḷic ceyalkaḷ, tiruvēṭattiṟku mūlamāṉa tirunīṟṟai
   vittakat tiṟampaṭa viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
   pōlit taṉmai illāta puṉita vēṭam, eṅkum viḷaṅka iṟaiyaruḷ vēṇṭum.
   tiruvēṭam kaṇṭa uḷḷam teḷivaṭaiyum. atteḷivu, ariya nalla aruḷ
   nāṭṭattai aḷikkum. pēṟu ataṉpiṉ peritāy viḷaiyum.
   vēṭattiṉ nuṭpam, nīṟṟiṉ teyvikam iraṇṭaiyum, kaṇapati perumāṉ auvaikku
   viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉārām.
   toṇṭartam perumai
   'mācuṟu maṉamē! kaṇṭaṉa maṟaiyum, uṇṭaṉa malamām,
   pūciṉa mācām, puṇarntaṉa piriyum,
   niṟaintaṉa kuṟaiyum, uyarntaṉa paṇiyum;
   piṟantaṉa iṟakkum, periyaṉa ciṟukkum.
   oṉṟuoṉṟu oruvaḻi nillā; aṉṟiyum,
   celvamoṭu piṟantōr, tēcoṭu tikaḻntōr,
   kalviyiṟ ciṟantōr, kaṭumtiṟal mikuntōr,
   koṭaiyiṟ polintōr, paṭaiyiṟ payiṉṟōr,
   kulattiṉ uyarntōr, nalattiṉil vantōr,
   eṉaiyarem kulattiṉar iṟantōr, aṉaiyavar
   pērum niṉṟila pōlum; tēriṉ
   nīyuma·tu aṟiti yaṉṟē! māyap
   pēyttēr pōṉṟu nīpparum uṟakkattuk
   kaṉavē pōṉṟu naṉavu peyarpeṟṟa
   māya vāḻkkaiyai matittuk kāyattaik
   kalliṉum valitāk karutip pollāt
   taṉmaiyar iḻivu cārntaṉai nīyum;
   aḻukkuṭaip pulaṉvaḻi iḻukkattiṉ oḻuki,
   naṉmaiyil tirinta puṉmaiyai ātaliṉ,
   vaḷaivāyt tūṇṭiliṉ uḷirai viḻuṅkum
   paṉmīṉ pōlavum,
   miṉṉuṟu viḷakkattu viṭṭil pōlavum,
   ācaiyiṉ paricat(tu) yāṉai pōlavum,
   ōcaiyiṉ viḷainta puḷḷup pōlavum,
   vīciya maṇattiṉ vaṇṭu pōlavum
   uṟuvatu uṇarāc ceṟuvaḻic cērntaṉai.
   nuṇnūl nūṟṟut taṉṉakap paṭukkum
   aṟivil kīṭattu nuntaḻip pōla
   ācaic caṅkilip pācat toṭarppaṭ(ṭu),
   iṭarkeḻu maṉattiṉo(ṭu) iyaṟṟuva(tu) aṟiyātu,
   kuṭarkeḻu ciṟaiyaip kuṟaṅkupu kiṭatti;
   kaṟavai niṉaitta kaṉṟeṉa iraṅki
   maṟavā maṉat(tu) mācaṟum aṭiyārk(ku)
   aruḷcuran(tu) aḷikkum aṟputak kūttaṉai,
   maṟaiyavar tillai maṉṟuḷ āṭum
   iṟaiyavaṉ eṉkilai eṉniṉain taṉaiyē!'
   - eṉṟu iṭaiyaṟātu uṇara uṇartti, maṉattiṉ maṭamaiyai māṟṟuvar.
   tiruntiya maṉam koṇṭu teyvak kaṇapatiyiṉ tiruvaṭi niṉaivar. tirikaraṇa
   vaḻipāṭṭil teḷivu peṟuvar. tam vacam keṭuvar; paravacap paṭuvar. petta
   nilai, mutti nilai iraṇṭilum, iṉpak kaṇapatimayamāka iruppar.
   pettattum taṉpaṇi yillai piṟattalāl
       muttattum taṉpaṇi yillai muṟaimaiyāl
       attaṟku iraṇṭum aruḷāl aḷittalāl
       pattip paṭṭōrkkup paṇiyoṉṟum illaiyē'

   - eṉṟu tikaḻum ivarkaḷai aṟivikkiṉṟatu tirumantiram.
   jīvaṉmuttar
   muṉṉaiya tavam kaṇṭu, valintu āḷvar varata kaṇapati. ataṉpiṉ niyati
   vaḻuvāmal āṉmā niṭṭai kūṭum. mutirnta innilaiyarai muttar eṉpar.
   ivarkaṭku ākāmiya cañcitam illai. eṉiṉum, aṭarnta pirārattuvam maṭṭum
   aḻivatillai. uṭal uḷḷavarai pirārattuvam; pirārattuvam uḷḷavarai uṭal.
   meyyuṇarntu niṭṭai mēviṉōrai, (peruṅkāyam irunta pāttirattil, illāta
   kālattum vācaṉai nīṅkāmal iruppatupōl), paḻakka vācaṉaiyāl pirārattuvam
   vantu paṟṟum. ap pirārattuva viṉaiyiṉ payaṉai aṉupavikkum poruṭṭu,
   kaṉinta avartam niṭṭai kalaiyum. avvamayam, kaṇmuṉ ulakam kāṭciyākum.
   niṭṭaiyil irunta iccai, aṟivu, ceyal mūṉṟum, uḷuṇarvu puṟam pōkum kālam
   pārttuk kāttirunta mummalamum, ivvamayam iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭiyai
   maṟappikkum.
   paramē pārttirunta muttarkku, ulakap poruḷkaḷaic cuṭṭiyaṟivatākiya
   viparīta uṇarvu mēṟpaṭum. ataṉāl meyyuṇarvu kīḻppaṭum. vīṇāṉa innilai
   piṟavikku vittākum. viruppum veṟuppum etaṉilum viḷaiyum. kaṭiṉamāṉa
   icceyalkaḷai viḷaivikkum kaṉma malam; mānilap poruḷkaḷaik kaṇmuṉ
   tōṟṟuvikkum māyā malam. aniyāya aṟivaip perukkum āṇava malam.
   yātu iṉik kati!
   uttamat tiyāṉattil oḻintu, caṟṟē annilai nīṅkiyatum mīṭṭum oṭṭiya
   malattaik kaṭṭaṟukka utavi tēvaiyeṉṟu jīvaṉmuttar kataṟuvar.
   ulakappaṟṟu mukkāl, kaṇapatip paṟṟu kāl pākam; ivarkaḷ mantatara
   aṉpiṉar. ulaka pācam araippākam, kaṇēcar vēṭkai araippākam; ivarkaḷ
   manta aṉpiṉar. ulaka vēṭkai kālpaṅku, mutalvar mēl aṉpu mukkāl paṅku;
   ivarkaḷ tīvira aṉpiṉar.
   ulakap paṟṟu aṟavē iṉṟi, muḻuvatum āṉaimukar kātalē ātal tīviratara
   aṉpu. kātal mikkavar kaṉinta aṭiyār. vittakarākiya avarkaṭkē uriyatu
   vīṭupēṟu. aṉpu ceyyum uyirum, aṉpum, aṉpu ceyappeṟum attimukarum eṉac
   collāl pirivu; eṉiṉum, pirivilāmal, kaṉinta attuvitamāy immūṉṟum
   kalantirukkum. karuṇai mikkavar atvaita kaṇapati aṭiyavar. ivarkaḷ,
   iṉiya aruḷmayamāki iruppārkaḷ.
   mēṟcoṉṉapaṭi kataṟum jīvaṉmuktar etirē kaṇapati aṭiyavar kāṭciyāvar.
   maṟatiyai viraintu māṟṟuvar; uṇarvuk kaṇṇai utavuvar. vaḻimuṟai
   kāṭṭuvar; oḷineṟiyil ūkkuvar; vāḻac ceyvar, jīvaṉ muttarai varata
   kaṇapati aṭiyavarkaḷ.
   inta arumaiyai aṟintatum, jīvaṉ muttarkaḷ, ūṉṟi aṭiyavaruṭaṉ uṟavu
   koḷvar; uṇarvu aṭiyariṭam uṟaippēṟa iruttaliṉ, ulaka aṉupava uṇarvu
   māṟum. mēṉmai anupūti uṇarvu mēliṭum. pāḻum pirārattuvam innilai kaṇṭu
   pātippatu illai.
   'cārpuṇarntu cārpukeṭa oḻukiṉ maṟṟaḻittuc
   cārtarā cārtarum nōy'
   - eṉum kuṟaḷ iṅku oppu nōkkat takum.
   evvaḷavu ciṟantavarkaḷ jīvaṉ muttarkaḷ! avarkaṭkē utavupavar
   aṭiyareṉil, piṟa uyirkaṭku ivarkaḷ ceyyum utavi pēca iyalumō! uttama
   aṭiyar irukkum iṭamē oḷimayam. ainteḻuttiṉ nātamayam. āṇṭapiṉ
   āṇṭavaṉum, intac cīvaṉ muttarai aṭiyariṭam uṟavāṭa viṭukiṉṟāṉ.
   'attaṉ āṇṭutaṉ aṭiyaril kūṭṭiya aticayam kaṇṭāmē' eṉum eṭṭām
   tirumuṟaiyilum, ikkuṟippu oṭṭiyuṇara uḷatu. paḻutta aṭiyavar eṉṟum
   payaṉ karutuvatu illai. avarkaḷ 'kūṭum aṉpiṉil kumpiṭalē aṉṟi vīṭum
   vēṇṭā viṟaliṉ viḷaṅkiṉār' eṉṟum, 'ceyviṉaiyum ceyvāṉum, ataṉpayaṉum,
   koṭuppāṉum meyvakaiyāl nāṉkākum vititta payaṉ' eṉṟum aruḷmoḻit tēvar
   aruḷiyapaṭi, parama avar paṇikku uriya payaṉaip paraṉē vaḻaṅkuvaṉ.
   'eṉkaṭaṉ paṇiceytu kiṭappatē
   taṉkaṭaṉ aṭiyēṉaiyum tāṅkutal'
   - eṉpatu aṭiyavar tiṭam.
   nīrāṉatu maṇṇai nilaikulaiyac ceyyum; anta nīrpperukkai, vaṭavaik kaṉal
   vaṟṟac ceyyum; akkaṉalaic caṇṭamārutam avittuviṭum; iṟutiyil akkāṟṟu,
   uyarnta viṇṇil oṭuṅki aṭaṅkum. iṅṅaṉam aimpūtaṅkaḷ nilaikulaiyum
   kālattum kalaṅkātār, paramaṉ malaraṭi maṟavātār, parama kaṇapati
   paktarkaḷ. 'toṇṭartam perumai collavum peritē!'
   'paluyir aṉaittaiyum okkap pārkkum ..
   teyvak kaṭavuḷ toṇṭar'
   -eṉṟu patiṉōrām tirumuṟai, paṇṭaiya toṇṭar perumaiyaip paṭikkiṉṟatu.
   inta arumaiyellām viḷaiya,
   'vēṭamum nīṟum viḷaṅka niṟuttik kūṭu meyttoṇṭar kuḻāttuṭaṉ kūṭṭi'ya
   pēraruḷai eṇṇik kuḻaikiṟatu pāṭṭi maṉam. itaṉai uṇarntu urukiṟatu
   nam uḷḷam.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   'Satsangatve nissangatvam
   nissangatve nirmohatvam
   nirmohatve nishchalatattvam
   nishcalatattve Jivanmuktih'
   From Satsanga comes non-attachment,
   from non-attachment comes freedom from delusion,
   which leads to self-settledness.
   From self-settledness comes Jivan Mukti.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   tiruvainteḻuttu
   mupporuḷ uṇarttum vāyppāṭu pōl irukkiṟatu,
   ainteḻuttu uṇmai.
   cikarattil pati uṇmai;
   vakarattil patiyiṉ aruḷkuṇam;
   yakarattil pacuvuṇmai;
   nakarattil maṟaippu;
   makarattil malam.
   ivai ellām pañcākkarattil veḷippaṭai. patiyai aṭaiyātapaṭi, āṉma aṟivai
   maṟaittup piṇittut taṉ vaḻi īrkkiṉṟatu, nakara, makaramākiya pācam.
   attaṭaikaḷ vakarattāl akalum. ataṉ piṉ patiyai aṭainta pacu pērāṉantap
   pēṟu peṟum.
   ainteḻuttu, emmantirattiṉum mutaṉmaiyatu. ōṅkārat tuṇaiyiṉṟi uyirkkiṉṟa
   perumai, ainteḻuttiṟkē uṇṭu. eḻutteḻuttāyp pirittu itaṟku viḷakkam
   kūṟuvatu eḷitu. meyññāṉam mēviṉarkkaṉṟi, itaṉ poruḷ evarkkum
   viḷaṅkuvatu illai. itaṉāl, arum poruḷ uṭaiyatu itu eṉṟu ataṉ nuṭpattaik
   kaṇapatiyē auvaiyārkku viḷakki aruḷ ceytārām.
   niyati vaḻuvātu ōtumaḷavu urimaiyam yām; uruvēṟa ēṟa, uḷḷattil ataṉ
   oḷiniḻal ūṉṟum. aruṭcatti tulaṅkum; meyyaṟivu viḷaṅkum.
   piṟar cevi kēṭka ōtuvatu cattam; metuvāka ōtuvatu mantam; maṉa aḷavāka
   eṇṇuvatu māṉatam eṉap peṟum. civañāṉikaṭku uriya māṉata naṭaiyē,
   auvaiyārkku aruḷap peṟṟatu.
   69. añcak karattiṉ arumporuḷ taṉṉai
   70. neñcak karuttiṉ nilaiyaṟi vittu
   patavurai:
   añcu akkarattiṉ - ainteḻuttiṉ,
   arumporuḷ taṉṉai - (uḷḷa) ariya poruḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷai,
   neñcak karuttiṉ - maṉa uṇarvil,
   nilai aṟivittu - nilai pēṟāmpaṭi uṇara vaittu
   (eṉṟavāṟu).
   'civāya namaeṉac cittam orukki
   avāyam aṟavē aṭimaiya tākkic
   civāya civaciva eṉṟeṉ ṟēcintai
   avāyam keṭaniṟka āṉantam āmē' - eṉpar tirumūlar.
   'mūccaic civāya eṉa uḷḷiḻuttu niṟutti nama: eṉṟu veḷiviṭu! allatu,
   civāya eṉa uyirppai vāṅkic civa eṉa viṭu! palanāḷ ceyyum payiṟciyil
   itayat tuṭippil iṉpam. āhā! ūṉṟi atai uṇar! palakālap payiṟciyil, tāṉē
   iyalpāka eḻum kuṇṭaliṉi, ātāra kamalaṅkaḷaiyaṭaintu, cahasrārattaic
   cārum.
   ataṉpiṉ ñāṉa viṇ nātam kēṭkum. muccuṭar tikutiku eṉa muṉṉē oḷirum.
   pantam ellām ataṉil ventu cāmparām. anta aṭaiyāḷamētāṉ ariya tirunīṟu.
   malam ellām nirmalamāṉa nilai itu. colvatu viḷaṅkukiṟatā? auvaiyē!
   aṟivil innilaiyai ilaṅkak koḷ!' eṉṟu, āṉaimukap perumāṉ aṟivuṟuttiya
   arumaiyē arumai.
   'tuñcalum tuñcal ilāta pōḻtiṉum
       neñcakam naintu niṉaimiṉ nāḷtoṟum
       vañcaka maṟṟaṭi vāḻtta vanta kūṟṟu
       añca utaittaṉa añce ḻuttumē'

   - eṉkiṟār āḷuṭaiya piḷḷaiyār.
   'naṟṟavā! uṉai nāṉ maṟakkiṉum
       collum nā namacci vāyavē'

   - eṉkiṟār cuntaramūrttikaḷ.
   'naṟṟuṇaiyāvatu namaccivāyavē!'
   - eṉkiṟār appar perumāṉ.
   na - maṟaikkum cakti,
   ma - malam,
   ci - civam,
   va - aruḷ,
   ya - āṉmā
   - eṉpatu potuvākak kuṟippiṭum puṉita naṭai.
   āṉmā maṟaippu nīṅki, malamakaṉṟu, tāṉēyāy, vakara aruḷai eyti,
   civattaic cārntu vīṭu peṟal, aintakkara nuṭpam.
   ulaka pantam uṟṟa poḻutu, uyirāṉatu pācamām maṟaippaiyum, malattaiyum
   cārntu niṟkum. pantam akaṉṟa kālattil, maṟaippaiyum malattaiyum akaṉṟu,
   aruḷai aṭaintu, civattaik kūṭi, perumpēṟu āṉa vīṭṭaip peṟum eṉṟu
   virittu viḷakka mutirnta aruḷnūlkaḷ peritum muyaṉṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu)
   akarāti yīreṇ kalanta paraiyum
       ukarāti taṉcakti yuḷḷoḷi yīcaṉ
       cikarāti tāṉciva vētamē kōṇa
       nakarāti tāṉmūla mantira naṇṇumē.

   - tirumantiram - 9-2700 - tūla pañcākkaram
   Seek the Seminal Mantra Nama Sivaya
   In the sixteen letters commencing with 'A'
   (Of the Shodasa Flower within)
   Is the Parai (Sakthi);
   In the Unmani Sakthi beginning with 'U'
   Is the light of Lord within;
   The Mantra commencing with letter 'Si' (Sivaya Nama)
   Is Siva and Vedas all,
   With letter 'Na' commencing
   That order reversed
   Is Nama Sivaya;
   That verily is the seminal Mantra;
   That Mantra do seek.
   vāyoṭu kaṇṭam itaya maruvunti
       āya iliṅka mavaṟṟiṉmē lēyavvāyt
       tūyatōr tuṇṭa mirumat takañcellal
       āyatī ṟāmaintō ṭāmeḻut tañcumē.

   - tirumantiram - 9-2701 - tūla pañcākkaram
   How Aum and the Five Letters are distributed in the Six Centres
   In the six Adharas (centres) within
   Are distributed the Five Letters and Aum;
   That comprise Aum Nama Sivaya
   In the Muladhara is Na
   In the Svadhishtana is Ma
   In the navel centre is Si
   In the Heart Centre is Va
   In the Throat Centre is Ya
   In the Eye-brow Centre - Ajna is Aum.
   civaṉcatti cīvaṉ ceṟumala māyai
       avañcērtta pāca malamaintakalac
       civaṉcatti taṉṉuṭaṉ cīvaṉār cēra
       avañcērtta pācam aṇuki lāvē.

   - tirumantiram - 9-2710 - cūkkuma pañcākkaram
   Si-Va-Ya-Na-Ma Brings Union of Jiva with SivaSakthi:
   The letters Si Va Ya Na Ma denote
   Siva, Sakthi, Jiva, Mala and Maya respective;
   Chant it, for the five Pasas-Mala to disappear;
   When with Siva and Sakthi, Jiva unites
   (That is when you say Si Va Ya)
   The harassing Pasas flee away.
   aṅkamum ākama vētama tōtiṉum
       eṅkaḷ pirāṉeḻut toṉṟil iruppatu
       caṅkaikeṭ ṭavveḻut toṉṟaiyuñ cātittāl
       aṅkarai cērnta aruṅkala māmē.

   - tirumantiram - 9-2710 - aticūkkuma pañcākkaram
   Chant 'Si' and Cross the River of Life:
   All that is spoken in Vedas, Vedangas and Agamas
   Are in my Lord's One Letter contained.
   Freed of doubts,
   If that One letter 'Si' is consummated,
   The boat of life reaches the lovely shore across.
   (English rendering - Dr.B.Natarajan)
   (patippāciriyar kuṟippu muṭintatu)
   vittaka vināyakar
   'ukattiṟku ātiyāt tēvarkku ātiyāy ulavāc
       cukattiṟku ātiyāyc curarkaḷukku īcarāyc cuṭarum
       makattiṟku īcarāyk kaṇaṅkaḷukku īcarāy matippōr
       akattiṟ kūṭiya kaṇēcaniṉ aṭiyiṇai toḻutām'

   eṉat tutitta mūvarkku muttoḻil puriyum mutaṉmai vaḻaṅkiya vittakar
   vināyakar.
   'uraiyāṉum uṇarvāṉum uṭalāṉum arivariya
       varaimārpum tiṇitōḷum tirumuṭiyum malarppatamum
       viraiyār kuṅkumak kalavai meyyaṇinta kōlamumāyk
       karaiyā neñcamum karaiyak kaṇṇetir niṉṟaṉai pōṟṟi'

   - eṉṟu ēttiya nāṉmukaṟku cemmaip paṭaippaic ceytaṟku uriya aṟivum
   toḻilumāṉa āṟṟalai aruḷiya vittakar vināyakar.
   'vētakā raṇamumnī vētamōr nāṉkumnī
       pātamōr nāṉkumnī pataṅkaḷōr nāṉkumnī
       pōtalai malaiyamār puvaṉakā raṇarumnī
       nātaniṉ perumaiyai navilaval lārevar!'

   - eṉa ēttiya viyācarkku, vētam tokuttu, purāṇa itikācaṅkaḷai virittu
   viḷakka utaviya vittakar vināyakar.
   'kuṇamoṭu kuṟipala koḷkaikoḷ ḷāyeṉil
       aṇimalar maṇameṉa ākkaiyiṉ uyireṉap
       paṇiyuyir aṟiveṉap payilumat tuvitamām
       kaṇapati niṉatiyal kāṇaval lārevar!'

   - eṉa ēttiṉōrtam tīrāppiṇi tīrtta vittakar teyva vināyakar.
   vātam kaṭantu palamāyai kaṉma
       vakaiyum kaṭantu avaicey
       ētam kaṭantu poruḷoṉṟu miṉṟi
       yeḻukiṉṟa mammar uyiriṉ
       pōtam kaṭantu, turiyam kaṭanta
       poruḷōyai yāṉum uṇarvēṉ
       vētam kaṭanta tirumēṉi koṇṭeṉ
       viḻikāṇa muṉṉar varalāl'

   - eṉat tutitta intiraṉuṭaiya ētam tavirtta vittakar eṉkiṟatu vināyaka
   purāṇam.
   uṭuṇṭi vināyakar, kajāṉaṉa vināyakar, kaṇapati, kaṇēcar, cintāmaṇi
   vināyakar, tūmakētu vināyakar, makōṟkaṭa vināyakar, vallapai vināyakar,
   vakratuṇṭa vināyakar, vikkiṉa vināyakar eṉac ciṟanta paṉṉiru
   tiruvavatāram ceytu, aṟputa pala āṭal nikaḻtti, aṉṟum iṉṟum ulakiṟku
   aruḷcey ammāṉai, vittaka vināyakar eṉṟu peyariṭṭu viḷittu vīḻntu
   vaṇaṅki, aṭaikkalam ākiṉṟār nam auvaip pirāṭṭiyār.
   eṅkum niṟaintāyē!
   viyāpakam
   malaimakaḷ tirumaṇattil, kaṇṇutal perumāṉ kaṇapatiyai vaḻipaṭṭār eṉṟu
   varalāṟṟu iruttaliṉ, vittaka vināyakartam toṉmai viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
   vētam tokutta viyācarkkup parama aruḷaip pālittār eṉṟa varalāṟṟāl,
   ennūlukkum mūlar vittaka vināyakar eṉum ceyti veḷiyākiṉṟatu.
   avvaḷavutāṉā? kaṇapati kāppu vaittē ennūlum ārampikkiṉṟa ceytiyai,
   akila ulakamum aṟiyum. iṉṟum perumpālōr, piḷḷaiyār cuḻi ūṉṟiyē eḻuta
   ārampippatum aṟiyat takum.
   kaṇapati vaḻipāṭu ceytapiṉ eccaṭaṅkum toṭaṅkuvatai, innāṭṭiṉ entak
   kuṭumpattiṉum aṟiyap peṟum. poykaik karai, marattaṭi, maḻai, nilā,
   veyil mutaliya iyaṟkai iṭaṅkaḷaiyēṉṟu, kōyilum iṉṟiyiruntu, vaḻiyē
   pōvār varuvārkkum aruḷum nilaiyil uḷatu, nam vittaka vināyakartam
   viyāpakam.
   vāṉ veḷi, itaya veḷiyākiya kōyilkaḷil, aṉpu apiṭēkamāka, ñāṉattai
   vaḻipāṭāka ēṟkiṉṟār, ñāṉikaḷ iṭattil anta vittaka vināyakar.
   ālamaram vittil aṭaṅkum; atupōl, piraṇavattil emmantiramum aṭaṅkum.
   anta ōm eṉum piraṇavamē vittaka vināyakariṉ vimala corupam. oṭitta
   kompiṉ iṭattil iruntu, piraṇavaccuḻi ārampippatai aṟivōm.
   eṇvakaic cittikaḷē parama kaṇapatikkup paṇimātar. piṟarāl tāḻttap
   peṟṟavaraiyum talainimira vaippavar ittaṟparar; ataṟku aṭaiyāḷamē, avar
   cirattiṉ mēl cūṭiyirukkum aṟuku.
   ciṟiya teru, periya vīti, naṭutteru, naṭuvīṭu mutaliya enta iṭattum,
   maṇ, kal, cāṇam, mañcaḷ, vellam, pañcalōkam, veḷḷi, poṉ mutaliya
   etaṉilum tiruvuruvai ēṟṟu iruppatu, vittaka vināyakar viyāpakattiṉ
   aṟikuṟi.
   pañca mūrttikaḷiṉum mēṟpaṭṭa (aruvam, uruvam, aruvuruvam eṉum
   navanilaiyum koṇṭa) turiya civattiṉ apēta avatārattai, vināyakar eṉṟu
   peyariṭṭu
   ēttukiṉṟatu vaitika camayam.
   viṣvaksēnar eṉum cēṉai nāyakar eṉkiṟatu vaiṣṇavam. putta nāyakar eṉṟu
   pōṟṟukiṉṟatu pautta matam. aruka nāyakar eṉṟu pēriṭṭu ārātikkiṉṟatu
   jaiṉamatam. iṅṅaṉam imayam mutal kumarivarai carvacamaya camaraca
   mūrttiyāṉa vittaka vināyakar, caiṉā, tipet, jappāṉ, viyaṭṉām, cāvakam
   mutaliya ettēcattiṉum kōyil koṇṭu irukkiṉṟār. pēraruḷ niṟainta
   ipperumāṉiṭam, aṉpiṉ uṇarvoṭu auvaip pirāṭṭiyār aṭaikkalam āvataiyum
   pāruṅkaḷ! eṉṉa arumai!
   aṭaikkalam
   71. tattuva nilaiyait tanteṉai āṇṭa
   72. vittaka vināyaka viraikaḻal caraṇē.
   patavurai:
   tattuva nilaiyait tantu - (tat tvam - tāṉē atu eṉṟu) uḷḷataṉ nilaiyai
   viḷakki uraittu,
   eṉai āṇṭa - eḷimaiyaṉai aṭimaiyākki āṭkoṇṭu,
   vittaka - mēlāṉa,
   vināyaka - oppārum mikkārum illāta muḻumutalē,
   virai kaḻal caraṇē - aruḷ maṇam niṟainta uṉatu tiruvaṭikaṭkē
   aṭaikkalam.
   tattuvam - uḷporuḷ. nilai - nilaiyāṉa vīṭṭai niṉaivuṟuttiyatu. eṉavē,
   nilaiyilā ulaka nilaikku māṟāṉa vīṭupēṟu eṉpatu, iṅkuk kuṟitta poruḷ.
   tantu - upatēcittu eṉum poruṭṭu.
   ulakiyal talaivar tōṉṟuvar; tam maṉa nilaikkut takkapaṭi makkaḷai
   ūkkuvar; ūkkutal upatēcattāl nalamum viḷaiyum; tīṅkum nēralām. iṟutiyil
   attalaivar maṟaivar. emmāṉ kaṇapati eṉṟum uḷḷavaṉ. vikaṟpam ilātāṉ.
   aṉṟum iṉṟum eṉṟum amalaṉ. apparamaṉ oruvaṉē āṉmāviṉ ciṟumaiyai
   aṟipavaṉ, ciṟumaiyai vilakkip perumaiyil uyirai piṟaṅkac ceypavaṉ ap
   perumāṉ.
   eṉai eṉpatu, ciṟumaiyuṭaiya eṉṉai eṉum poruṭṭu. vittaka eṉpatu,
   viḷiyeṉalumām.
   avaṉiṭamaṉṟi vēṟu evariṭam aṭaikkalam āvatu? itaiyaṟintē 'viraikaḻal
   caraṇē' eṉṟār. patiṉāṟām aṭiyil, 'āṭkoḷa vēṇṭit tāyāy eṉakkut
   tāṉeḻuntaruḷi'ṉāṉ eṉṟār. ataṟku ēṟpa, āṭkoḷḷaṟku uḷa aruḷ ceyalkaḷaic
   ciṟitu ciṟitākac ceyta pēraraṟiviṉ perumaiyaiyeṇṇi, 'vittaka' eṉṟār.
   vittakam eṉpatu oṉṟiṟkum payaṉ illāta taṉṉaiyum oru poruḷākki,
   aṭiñāṉattaiyum, aruḷaiyum muṟaiyē taricikka aruḷiya āṟṟalai
   uṇarttiyatu.
   'cītak kaḷapac centāmaraip pūmpātac cilampu' eṉṟu tiruvaṭip perumaiyai
   ārampittu, nūl niṟaivilum, 'viraikaḻal caraṇē' eṉa muṭikkum maṅkala
   aṟputam maṟakkat takuvataṉṟu.
   tiruvaṭi - corupa nilaiyil paripūraṇa ñāṉam;
   paripūraṇa ñāṉa attaṉ aruḷiya parama upatēcattai, uṇarntu urukip
   pāṭiya upaniṭatam itu. ātaliṉ, civañāṉa vēṭkaiyarkkup pala nuṭpaṅkaḷai
   niṉaivuṟuttum innūl eṉpatum iyaṟkaitāṉē!
   kaḻal, vīra aṭaiyāḷamāṉa tiruvaṭiyaṇi; atu tāṉiyāku peyarāy,
   ataṉaiyaṇinta tiruvaṭikaḷai eṇṇac ceytatu.
   'ayarā aṉpiṉ araṉ kaḻal celumē' eṉpatu civañāṉapōtam.
   maṇam poruntiya tiruvaṭi malar eṉpār, 'viraikaḻal' eṉṟār. virai - teyva
   maṇam; 'iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷ ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum kaṭantu niṉṟaṉa;
   āyiṉum, aṉparkku aṇiyavāy, avar iṭṭa naṟumalarāṉa veṟi kamaḻum eṉpatu
   pōtara, veṟiyār kaḻal eṉṟār' - eṉṟu tirukkōvaiyāruḷ pērāciriyar kūṟiya
   urainayam, ūṉṟi iṅku uṇarat takum. āṉaimukap paramaṉ aṭik kamalaṅkaṭkē
   aṭiyēṉ aṭaikkalam eṉum poruḷil, 'viraikaḻal caraṇē' eṉa nūlai muṭikkum
   arumaiyē arumai!
   ñāṉam paktiyākiya cātaṉaṅkaḷ, tiruvaṭi niḻalil iruttip pēriṉpap pēṟṟaip
   peṟac ceyyum. ātaliṉ, tiruvaṭi vaḻipāṭu, emmatamum cammatikkum
   iṉpanaṭaiyuṭaiyatu. innuṭpam ōrntu,
   'māyai taṉaiyutaṟi valviṉaiyaic cuṭṭumalam
       cāya amukkiyaruḷ tāṉeṭuttu nēyattāl
       ānanta vāritiyil āṉmāvait tāṉaḻuttal
       tāṉentai yārpātan tāṉ'

   - eṉa ōtukiṉṟatu uṉmai viḷakka nūl.
   vētam kaṇapatiyiṉ nātam. ākamaṅkaḷ, apperumāṉai ārātikka uraitta
   aruḷmuṟaikaḷ. upaniṭataṅkaḷ vikkinēcar uyirppu. ōṅkāram avar tirumēṉi.
   akattum puṟattum vāḻa vaḻi kaṟpikkum anta varata kaṇapatiyiṉ
   aruḷākkattai, virittu inta aruḷ nūl viḷampukiṉṟatu.
   uruviṟ ciṟiyatu; eṉiṉum, poruḷ nuṭpattiṟ periya innūliṟ piravēcittu,
   ciṟṟaṟiviṟkut tōṉṟiya aḷavil utitta icciṟṟuraiyai, catura kaṇapatiyiṉ
   tiruvaṭikaḷil camarppikkiṉṟōm.
   cānti
   malaimaṭantai aruḷ pālā!
   matamikuntiḻi kapōlā!
   alakil aṉpar paṇi kālā!
   aruḷ curantiṭu vicālā!
   ilakukiṉṟa paṇimārpā!
   iṉiya mantra corūpā!
   poliperuṅ karuṇaiyāḷā!
   iṉiya vināyaka emmāṉē!
   iṉpamayamāṉa niṉṉaruḷāl 'vāḻka inta mantira aruḷnūl!' eṉṟu varata
   kaṇapatiyai vāyārap pāṭi, maṉamāra eṇṇik kaikuvittuk kumpiṭuvam nām!
   vinākar akaval virivurainimalaṉ tiruvaruḷ utaviya aḷavil niṟaivu
   peṟukiṟatu.
   ōm
